# QuotesDone — Backend Audit (2026-09-14) — Part 1 of 2: what was measured

> **Status of this document.** This is the completed AUDIT half. Eleven readers measured the estate read-only against production on 2026-09-14, and eleven gap analyses were derived from them — all of that is here, unabridged. The second half — adversarial verification of each material finding, the verdict on which seats are still missing, and the one-year plan with tasks per seat — was paused before it finished, on the founder's instruction, to save the session limit. It resumes from cache; nothing here will be re-measured.
>
> Every number states how it was obtained. Findings are the readers' own and have **not yet been through the refutation pass**, so treat the severity labels as the readers' claims. Where the OpenRouter hold binds, it is noted once per dimension and not argued.

## The estate in one table

| | count |
|---|---|
| Dimensions measured | 11 |
| Things inventoried | 225 — built and exercised 85 · built, never exercised 73 · partial 44 · broken 13 · absent 10 |
| Material findings | 126 — blocks revenue 17 · blocks autonomy 22 · degrades 59 · cosmetic 28 |
| Gaps to autonomous profit | 146 — of which founder-dependent 37 |

## 1. What blocks revenue and autonomy today (readers' findings, unverified, severity-ordered)

| 1 | BLOCKS REVENUE | wiring | The non-response ladder the receipts promise (chase at working day 3, CEO decides at day 5, refund at non-response) has no clock: fn_refund_ladder_sweep's only caller is fn_company_self_knowledge_sync, which itself has no caller; it is not in resilience_ping; 0 runs. When the first reveal lands, the promise in trg_reveal_receipt's emails will not be kept by anything automatic. | yes |
| 2 | BLOCKS REVENUE | supplier_funnel | supplier_capabilities.confirmed_at has NO writer anywhere: capability_agent, its only writer, was deleted in commit 182308b and is not deployed, while supplier_api.capability_confirm and the portal's CapabilityConfirmPanel still call it. fn_match_capability's first WHERE clause is confirmed_at IS NOT NULL, so the capability shortlist can never return a row until a writer exists. |  |
| 3 | BLOCKS REVENUE | supplier_funnel | leads.verified DOES have a writer, but only one path: supplier_api.save_profile sets verified=true and approval_status='approved' when auto_approve_suppliers=true and a licence path or number is present and the expiry is not past. That path requires a claimed lead (claimed_by=email), and 0 leads are claimed, so verified=0 on all 22,264 rows and both matchers (fn_is_matchable) return nothing. The gate checks presence of a licence file/number only — not the founder's six. |  |
| 4 | BLOCKS REVENUE | supplier_funnel | enqueue_supplier_invites cannot run even when the founder opens outreach: after the v_go check it calls public.fn_email_sendable(l.email), and no routine of that name exists in pg_proc. The first real run will raise 42883 and queue nothing. | yes |
| 5 | BLOCKS REVENUE | supplier_funnel | How many suppliers can receive a job today: ZERO, by every path. What has to happen for the first one to become matchable, in order: (1) a lead becomes claimed (claim_token link via claim_lookup, or create_company) — needs either the invite path (enqueue_supplier_invites fixed for fn_email_sendable AND both founder flags true) or a supplier arriving at /en/portal unaided; (2) the supplier verifies the login email (login_otp / kyc), SMS-verifies a UAE mobile (kyc.send_sms/verify_sms — Twilio must be configured), uploads a readable trade licence (license_extract via ai_gateway — OpenRouter hold binds here once) and a company profile, ticks terms, and calls save_profile → verified=true, approval_status='approved', registration_submitted_at set; kyc.accept_terms → compliance_archive writes signed_terms_path; (3) something writes supplier_capabilities.confirmed_at for that lead — impossible today because capability_agent is gone; a replacement writer (or a direct confirm routine behind supplier_api) must exist and the row must carry a role with executes=true; (4) an inquiry must call fn_match_capability — nothing calls it today; customer_api calls match_suppliers_kw, which needs only step (2). So the shortest path to the first matchable supplier through the path that is actually wired is steps (1)+(2) with match_suppliers_kw; the capability path additionally needs (3)+(4). | yes |

All 56 categories carry required fields, so a request that names a trade (English keyword map, or fn_kb_route_decide for Arabic) is held at needs_info indefinitely and 'Send to suppliers' never appears; create_inquiry independently refuses without a ready intake row (428 intake_required). ## 2. The gaps to a company that runs itself and earns, by the seat that owns closing them

### ceo — 25 gap(s)

| gap | why it blocks autonomous profit | effort | founder? | dimension |

| fn_match_capability has no caller anywhere (no DB routine, no edge function, no src reference beyond a comment); customer_api route_inquiry calls match_suppliers_kw (keyword + fn_is_matchable, no confirmed_at gate). Two matchers exist and neither is designated canonical. | The confirmed-scope, authority-aware matcher the funnel is built toward is dead code on the request path; the live path matches on keywords only, so effort spent on capability confirmation buys nothing until a matcher decision is made and wired. | days |  | Supplier side |
| The founder's mandatory-six ruling of 7 Sep is not in public.founder_instruction; its only record is the session memory file supplier-verification-mandatory-six.md. | Per the organised-like-an-auditor rule a decision with no home is lost; the verification standard the seats must enforce cannot be read by any agent from the database. | days |  | Supplier side |

No resilience_ping tick, no GitHub workflow, no cron row calls it. | Anything a seat escalates to the CEO (a case decision, a refund over a grant ceiling, a weekly report to acknowledge, a founder command) waits until a human opens a session and calls ceo_agent. Path is pull-at-report-time plus piggyback on the next expense post; no event tells the accountant a reveal happened. — `stripe_webhook reveal branch L231-268 (inserts transactions; does NOT call fn_gl_sync_business — only the supplier-dues branch L181 does); fn_gl_sync_business posts reveal_fee journals but is called only from fn_expense_post_to_gl; fn_accountant_weekly_report_figures reads transactions directly` |

No code reads handoff_targets to move work (grep supabase/functions: only it_engineer/index.ts L145 selects the column for display). — `public.org_role` |

**Findings**

- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — The non-response ladder the receipts promise (chase at working day 3, CEO decides at day 5, refund at non-response) has no clock: fn_refund_ladder_sweep's only caller is fn_company_self_knowledge_sync, which itself has no caller; it is not in resilience_ping; 0 runs. When the first reveal lands, the promise in trg_reveal_receipt's emails will not be kept by anything automatic.
  - evidence: pg_proc prosrc caller scan; resilience_ping/index.ts tick list; agent_runs 0; trg_reveal_receipt body cites v_chase/v_window
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY · founder** — The CEO seat receives every upward channel (support_ceo_report, ceo_task, refunds, seat board) only when a human session issues an HTTP call to ceo_agent; nothing schedules it. 2 reports have sat unaccepted since 9 Sep and 2 ceo_tasks open since 8 Sep. A channel with a reader that only runs when the founder opens a session is a founder dependency, not an autonomous loop.
  - evidence: agent_runs fn:ceo_agent = 4 all-time (2 OK, 2 401), none after 2026-09-09 20:06; resilience_ping and .github/workflows never call ceo_agent; support_ceo_report accepted_at NULL x2; ceo_task state open x2
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY · founder** — Work assigned to seats is never picked up. org_tasks holds 13 assigned rows (attempts 0, last_run_at NULL); no seat runtime reads org_tasks; fn_org_task_assign and fn_agent_action_work_sweep have 0 runs and no live caller. The CEO cannot assign work to a seat at all: ceo_task has no assignee column, and org_tasks rows are self-assigned by the writer at insert.
  - evidence: org_tasks row dump; grep supabase/functions -> 0 readers; agent_runs 0 for org_task_assign/action_worker; ceo_task columns
- **Degrades** — sales_engineer -> customer_support and customer_support -> sales_engineer have no channel. org_role declares the handoff both ways, but no table, routine or edge code moves a question, a case or a fact between them; fn_seat_for_surface only decides which seat answers a surface.
  - evidence: org_role.handoff_targets; grep sales_engineer/index.ts for customer_support -> 1 comment line; site_assistant references sales_engineer only in fn_seat_for_surface routing (L209-218)
- **Degrades** — The accountant does not learn of a reveal payment when it happens. stripe_webhook's reveal branch inserts transactions and queues customer/supplier receipts but never calls fn_gl_sync_business (only the supplier-dues branch does); reveal_fee journals post only when the next expense is posted (fn_expense_post_to_gl -> fn_gl_sync_business). The weekly figures read transactions at report time. Unexercised: transactions = 0.
  - evidence: stripe_webhook/index.ts L181 vs L231-268; fn_gl_sync_business routine callers = fn_expense_post_to_gl only; gl_journal source reveal_fee 0
- **Degrades** — The CEO's first weekly whole-company report (W36) is stranded: generated 9 Sep 17:47 by a direct call, status 'generated', never delivered, and fn_ceo_weekly_report_tick keys on the current week so it will never retry W36. The founder asked for this report on 9 Sep and has not received one.
  - evidence: ceo_weekly_report id 1: founder_submitted_at NULL, notification_id NULL, delivery_attempts 0; tick body: skips only when founder_submitted_at is not null for date_trunc('week', today-7)
- **Degrades** — customer_support never consults its CEO standing grants. 4 grants exist for it (conversation, document, notification, refund) but wa_inbound and site_assistant never call fn_agent_may_act; the 'checked the standing grant' string in their p_attempted arrays is text, not a call. The other three grantee seats do call it.
  - evidence: ceo_standing_grant rows; grep fn_agent_may_act -> accountant, it_engineer, sales_engineer only; wa_inbound L1021 literal
- **Cosmetic · founder** — it_engineer has no path to the CEO. org_reporting_edge has no it_engineer row (so fn_support_report_to_ceo would refuse it), escalation_targets=['founder'] only, and the CEO sees IT only as counts in fn_ceo_seat_board / fn_ceo_weekly_figures. This matches the founder's 9 Sep instruction that IT reports to him directly; recorded as a structural fact, not proposed for change.
  - evidence: org_reporting_edge 3 rows; org_role.it_engineer; fn_ceo_weekly_figures health block
- **Cosmetic** — Duplicate, never-closed accountant tasks: fn_founder_mail_route opens an org_task for every 'expense_logged' signal (3 rows: Vercel 49.9 USD, GoDaddy 93.8 AED, Github 67.45 USD) while the accountant books the same receipt through resend_inbound/fn_expense_post_to_gl in the same minute. Nothing reads or closes the task, so the CEO board will show accountant open_tasks growing with every receipt.
  - evidence: org_tasks ids 578943/578946/578948 decided_by fn_founder_mail_route vs expense_entries rows at the same timestamps
- **Cosmetic** — Declared org graph points at ghosts: ceo.handoff_targets = chief_of_staff, chief_performance_officer (not seats); founder_mail_policy owner_role names customer_success_director, customer_onboarding, marketplace_director (removed). Runtime falls back (fn_founder_mail_route regex -> customer_support/ceo) so nothing breaks, but the auditor-facing tables describe an org that does not exist.
  - evidence: org_role.ceo.handoff_targets; founder_mail_policy rows customer_escalation/refund_complaint/customer_welcome/rfq_sla_breach
- **Cosmetic** — ceo_daily_digest.delivered_at is never written (all 5 rows NULL, status 'submitted') although the 5 founder_brief mails are status 'sent'. The row understates its own delivery; any reader of delivered_at will report 0 delivered.
  - evidence: ceo_daily_digest rows; fn_ceo_daily_digest_submit body sets submitted_at only; notifications 21285..21413 sent
- **Cosmetic** — Three department-era channels are dead but present: exec_reports (325, all 'obsolete'), board_reports (183), agent_chats (1414 rows keyed to removed departments), plus 8 empty channel tables (handoff_contract with from_dept/to_dept, support_case, support_notification, agent_pending_actions, channel_directive, founder_directive, ceo_decision_log, finance_reports). No writes since 7 Sep.
  - evidence: row counts and max(created_at) per table
- **Cosmetic · founder** — Where the OpenRouter hold binds on this dimension: only the LLM reply paths of wa_inbound and site_assistant; every inter-seat channel measured here is deterministic SQL/edge code and is unaffected by the hold. Noted once.
  - evidence: channel routines are plpgsql; the seat 'assist' actions call fn_*_assist retrieval routines

*Not measured:* Per-routine call counts: pg_stat_user_functions is empty (track_functions off), so 'never called' claims rest on row evidence, agent_runs, and prosrc/grep caller scans, not on execution counters.; Which of the two successful ceo_agent calls on 9 Sep were 'inbox' vs 'board' vs 'command': agent_runs.counts records only path/method/http.; Whether the founder opened or read any of the 29 admin emails (no open/click telemetry in notifications).; Edge function runtime logs (query_logs) were not read; only agent_runs was used for liveness.; The identity of the external scheduler hitting resilience_ping every ~15 min (comments say Vercel cron; only the daily GitHub Action at 07:17 UTC was verified in the repo).; The OS dashboard / portal UI rendering of founder_alerts channel 'os' rows and of fn_ceo_seat_board (UI not driven per the founder's instruction).; Content of fn_ceo_daily_digest and fn_founder_brief bodies beyond which tables they reference (only prosrc ilike scans were run).; scripts/*.mjs callers of fn_agent_context / fn_org_task_assign (30+ files matched the name grep; most are RETIRED checks or staging blueprints; none was executed or read in full).; Actual graph that would be drawn from agent_chats (legacy, 1414 rows) — treated as dead and not analysed.; ADJACENCY LIST (measured, live edges only; -> means a row is written AND a reader exists): customer_support -> ceo [support_ceo_report via wa_inbound/site_assistant; wired, 0 rows; reader ceo_agent inbox, manual]; customer_support -> customer_support [org_tasks knowledge_gap, 3 rows, no reader]; accountant -> ceo [support_ceo_report 2 rows unaccepted; ceo_case_decision path 0 rows]; accountant -> founder [accountant_weekly_report 1 sent, accountant_spend_report 4 sent]; sales_engineer -> sales_engineer [org_tasks knowledge_gap 10 rows, no reader; sales_decision_log 96 self-log]; it_engineer -> founder [it_engineer_handover 18 sent]; it_engineer -> ceo [none; CEO reads it_watch_finding counts only]; ceo -> founder [ceo_daily_digest 5 sent; ceo_weekly_report 0 sent]; ceo -> accountant/sales_engineer/it_engineer [ceo_standing_grant read at call time via fn_agent_may_act]; ceo -> customer_support [grants exist, never read]; ceo -> any seat (work) [none: ceo_task has no assignee, org_tasks has no seat reader]; founder -> ceo [ceo_command 2 -> ceo_task 2 open]; system -> accountant [org_tasks expense_logged 3, unread]; sales_engineer <-> customer_support [none]; stripe -> accountant [transactions pulled weekly; no push]; notifications -> any seat [0 rows ever].

**Findings**

- **BLOCKS REVENUE** — supplier_capabilities.confirmed_at has NO writer anywhere: capability_agent, its only writer, was deleted in commit 182308b and is not deployed, while supplier_api.capability_confirm and the portal's CapabilityConfirmPanel still call it. fn_match_capability's first WHERE clause is confirmed_at IS NOT NULL, so the capability shortlist can never return a row until a writer exists.
  - evidence: supabase/functions/capability_agent missing; git show --stat 182308b lists capability_agent/index.ts -354; list_edge_functions has no capability_agent; supplier_api/index.ts L312 fetches functions/v1/capability_agent; supplier_capabilities confirmed_at set = 0 of 15,305; supplier_capability_confirmation = 0 rows; pg_proc scan: no DB routine writes either table.
- **BLOCKS REVENUE** — leads.verified DOES have a writer, but only one path: supplier_api.save_profile sets verified=true and approval_status='approved' when auto_approve_suppliers=true and a licence path or number is present and the expiry is not past. That path requires a claimed lead (claimed_by=email), and 0 leads are claimed, so verified=0 on all 22,264 rows and both matchers (fn_is_matchable) return nothing. The gate checks presence of a licence file/number only — not the founder's six.
  - evidence: supplier_api/index.ts L551-580, L586; pricing_config.auto_approve_suppliers=true; leads verified=0, claimed_by set=0, approval_status set=0; fn_is_matchable requires coalesce(p_verified,false); no DB routine assigns verified (pg_proc scan).
- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — enqueue_supplier_invites cannot run even when the founder opens outreach: after the v_go check it calls public.fn_email_sendable(l.email), and no routine of that name exists in pg_proc. The first real run will raise 42883 and queue nothing.
  - evidence: pg_get_functiondef(enqueue_supplier_invites) contains '(public.fn_email_sendable(l.email)->>''ok'')::boolean'; select proname from pg_proc where proname ilike '%sendable%' = none. Today the routine exits early because invite_enabled='false' and supplier_outreach_golive='false', which is why the defect has not surfaced.
- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — How many suppliers can receive a job today: ZERO, by every path. What has to happen for the first one to become matchable, in order: (1) a lead becomes claimed (claim_token link via claim_lookup, or create_company) — needs either the invite path (enqueue_supplier_invites fixed for fn_email_sendable AND both founder flags true) or a supplier arriving at /en/portal unaided; (2) the supplier verifies the login email (login_otp / kyc), SMS-verifies a UAE mobile (kyc.send_sms/verify_sms — Twilio must be configured), uploads a readable trade licence (license_extract via ai_gateway — OpenRouter hold binds here once) and a company profile, ticks terms, and calls save_profile → verified=true, approval_status='approved', registration_submitted_at set; kyc.accept_terms → compliance_archive writes signed_terms_path; (3) something writes supplier_capabilities.confirmed_at for that lead — impossible today because capability_agent is gone; a replacement writer (or a direct confirm routine behind supplier_api) must exist and the row must carry a role with executes=true; (4) an inquiry must call fn_match_capability — nothing calls it today; customer_api calls match_suppliers_kw, which needs only step (2). So the shortest path to the first matchable supplier through the path that is actually wired is steps (1)+(2) with match_suppliers_kw; the capability path additionally needs (3)+(4).
  - evidence: Column counts (claimed 0, verified 0, confirmed_at 0), live definitions of fn_is_matchable/fn_match_capability/match_suppliers_kw, supplier_api L256-320/L486-616, kyc/index.ts L179-233, compliance_archive L174, customer_api L220, absence of capability_agent and fn_email_sendable, flags invite_enabled/supplier_outreach_golive='false'.
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — Nothing drives the supplier loop on a clock: pg_cron has one inactive job, GitHub Actions run only resilience_ping (agent ticks), kb-embed-backfill and project-parity. enqueue_supplier_invites, doc_extract's queue drainer and fn_reconcile_doc_blocks (the daily licence-expiry suspender that fn_is_matchable's comment relies on) have no caller — fn_reconcile_doc_blocks' only textual reference is that comment.
  - evidence: select * from cron.job → 1 row wa_flush active=false; .github/workflows grep; pg_proc callers of enqueue_supplier_invites = none, of fn_reconcile_doc_blocks = only the comment inside fn_is_matchable; invite_schedule rows stop at 2026-09-07 with sent=0.
- **Degrades** — The 171 'invited' leads have no delivery record: notifications holds 0 supplier_invite rows (purged), the first 62 invitations carried no link, 10 addresses are now suppressed, and invite_status='sent'/'bounced' is a self-written column. The stage-1 count is a claim, not a measurement.
  - evidence: notifications type='supplier_invite' = 0, min(created_at)=2026-09-07; invite_schedule sum(sent)=171; scripts/sql/fiftyseven_suppliers_were_invited_to_click_a_button_that_was_not_there.sql header; leads invited_at not null joined to email_suppressions = 10.
- **Degrades** — The founder's mandatory six is implemented as fn_supplier_mandatory_check but is not on the approval or matching path: save_profile approves on licence presence alone, fn_is_matchable reads only verified/banned/blocked/approval/closed/strikes, and the checker is called only from WhatsApp/support document routines. Email verification in the checker reads the scrape column leads.email_verified, not owner_email_verified (the OTP flag).
  - evidence: fn_supplier_mandatory_check live def; callers via pg_proc scan = fn_wa_fact_pack, fn_wa_attach_document, fn_support_receive_document, fn_support_answer_identity_challenge; supplier_api save_profile L551-579 has no call to it.
- **Degrades** — Additional gate columns with no live writer, beyond the three already known: supplier_capabilities.confirmed_version and excluded_trades (writer was capability_agent); leads.verification_status (default 'unverified' on all rows, nothing assigns it); leads.lifecycle_stage (set by a 29 Jul backfill migration; no trigger, routine or function writes it now); leads.approved_at (no writer in DB or edge code — the only approved_at writes found are on other tables); leads.contact_name and owner_phone_verified are written only from the portal/kyc paths that need a claimed lead. leads.service_emirates DOES have writers (supplier_api save_profile L492, process_docs L423) but only on a claimed lead, so 0 rows carry it.
  - evidence: pg_proc regex scan per column (n_routines=0 for confirmed_at, confirmed_version, excluded_trades, verification_status, lifecycle_stage, approved_at, contact_name, owner_phone_verified, service_emirates, trade_license_no, doc_extracted_at); repo grep per column; column counts on leads.
- **Degrades** — Even after confirmed_at and verified get writers, fn_match_capability's role and authority gates narrow the derived pool: only 2,244 of 15,305 supplier_capabilities rows carry a role whose taxonomy_role.executes is true, and 0 rows hold any authority_approvals while 3 taxonomy trades require one — RFQs in those 3 trades will match nobody.
  - evidence: count of sc rows with executes role = 2,244; count(authority_approvals<>'{}')=0; count(taxonomy_trade where cardinality(authority_required)>0)=3.
- **Cosmetic** — supplier_api still fetches two deleted functions on every save_profile: profile_ai (aiSummarize) and supplier_review (aiReview). Both are swallowed by try/catch, so capability_summary/service_emirates enrichment and ai_review_notes silently never happen. The management 'supplier_registered' email is still composed and queued regardless.
  - evidence: supplier_api/index.ts L123, L126 fetch functions/v1/profile_ai and supplier_review; neither in repo (182308b deleted them) nor in list_edge_functions.
- **Cosmetic** — The mandatory-six ruling (7 Sep) is not in public.founder_instruction; the table starts 9 Sep and has no supplier/verification subject. Per the organised-like-an-auditor rule a decision with no home is a decision that will be lost.
  - evidence: founder_instruction count=42, min(said_at)=2026-09-09, 0 rows matching mandatory/UAE mobile/supplier/verif/invit; memory file supplier-verification-mandatory-six.md is the only record.

*Not measured:* Live behaviour of either portal — not driven (founder: live UI tests are not information); all flow claims come from reading code and column state.; Whether send_outbox actually delivers today for a new invite: agent_runs send_outbox last ran 2026-09-13 and 32 notifications read 'sent', but no supplier_invite has been composed since the purge, so delivery of that template is untested.; Resend-side status of the 171 historical invitations: their notifications rows were purged and provider_message_id was only added 9 Sep; no provider record is reachable from the database.; Twilio Verify configuration (twilio_api_key_sid/secret/verify_service_sid) and Resend key presence — credential values were not read; the mobile-verification step is assumed available, not proven.; Why invite_schedule has rows for 3–7 Sep with sent=0 while no cron or routine calls enqueue_supplier_invites — the historical caller (likely a pg_cron job removed before 7 Sep) was not traced.; Which routine originally derived the 15,305 supplier_capabilities rows (no live writer found); their quality against the taxonomy was not sampled beyond the role/authority counts.; The 8 supplier-kind used email_verifications rows: which addresses and whether any correspond to a real (non-probe) company — auth.users was not queried.; Edge functions supplier_ack and supplier_catalog_ingest were listed but not read.; fn_reconcile_doc_blocks, fn_register_document, fn_supplier_registration_state and fn_supplier_expiries bodies were not read; only existence and callers were checked.; supplier_perf / supplier_score contents that feed fn_match_capability's track score were not inspected.

Supplier charged: fee + AED 250 + 9% p.a. simple interest — base differs by routine: fn_refund_amount_aed interest on fee only; fn_supplier_nonresponse_penalty interest on fee+250. Example Q=10,000, 30 days: 300 + 250 + 2.22 (fee base) = 552.22 vs 300 + 250 + 4.07 (fee+admin base) = 554.07 | pg_get_functiondef of both routines; decision_policy and pricing_config values |

Over the 11 months Sep 2026-Jul 2027: AED 90,909 fee/month = AED 3.03M revealed quote value/month (e.g. 303 reveals/month at AED 10k, or 30/month at AED 100k). Current run rate: 0. The VAT threshold (375k) sits at 37.5% of the target: registration becomes mandatory on the preceding-12-months test before the target is reached; after registration reveal_invoice back-computes 5% out of the fee (fee/1.05), cutting net by ~4.8% unless the fee is re-priced. | Arithmetic on pricing_config and gl_settings; the target itself is from the founder's 11 Aug 2026 instruction (memory), not from founder_instruction (no row matches '1M'/'million') |

**Findings**

- **BLOCKS REVENUE** — No revenue can be taken because the funnel that produces a reveal is empty end to end: 0 verified suppliers out of 22,264 leads, 0 customers, 0 inquiries, 0 quotes. Every money-in function is code that has only ever been hit by a probe. The first dirham depends, in order, on: (1) at least one supplier passing the mandatory-six verification (verified=true stamps founding_rank; category_open_min_suppliers=10 in pricing_config gates a category), (2) a customer with a customers row (stripe_checkout returns 403 'No customer profile' without one) posting an inquiry, (3) that supplier quoting, (4) the customer reaching stripe_checkout from the /me portal (known incomplete), (5) the live webhook delivering checkout.session.completed (enabled_events unmeasured).
  - evidence: customers/inquiries/quotes/transactions all 0 rows; leads verified=false ×22,264, claimed_by NULL ×22,264, invited_at on 171; notifications since 7 Sep: 0 to customers; stripe_checkout L31-32, L47; pricing_config.category_open_min_suppliers=10
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — The refund ladder has no clock. fn_refund_ladder_sweep (chase at working day 3, final call at day 5, refund request on silence) was scheduled as pg_cron job refund_ladder_sweep_hourly; cron.job now holds one inactive job and resilience_ping, which inherited every other orphaned tick, does not call it. The founder's 6 Sep promise ('after 3 days ... chase and on the 5th day ... final call ... we issue the refund') cannot advance on its own once a reveal exists.
  - evidence: scripts/sql/the_refund_ladder_is_five_working_days…sql L635-654 creates the cron; select * from cron.job → 1 row wa_flush active=false; grep resilience_ping/index.ts for refund\|ladder → none; pg_proc bodies calling fn_refund_ladder_sweep → only fn_company_self_knowledge_sync (text)
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY · founder** — A refund cannot be executed by any agent. The only code that POSTs /v1/refunds is refunds_api action 'resolve', which requires fn_control_mode_require(actor_email,'refund_resolve') to allow a live human Control Mode session (control_mode_action.refund_resolve requires_control=true). fn_refund_execute_authorized (the typed executor that reads the CEO's 'ceo_authorized' state) has zero callers, and fn_ceo_decide_refund has zero callers (ceo_agent writes refunds.refund_decision_id directly). This contradicts the 8 Sep ruling that all approvals including refunds are the CEO's and nothing goes to the founder: money leaves only when a human holds a session.
  - evidence: refunds_api/index.ts L130-152; control_mode_action rows; pg_proc search: 0 bodies reference fn_refund_execute_authorized or fn_ceo_decide_refund; ceo_agent/index.ts L275-281; founder_instruction 1 and 7 (status done) describe CEO-confirms-then-accountant-issues
- **Degrades** — Reveal-fee revenue never reaches the ledger on its own. fn_gl_sync_business posts 4000 Reveal Fee Revenue (net of the modelled Stripe fee) but stripe_webhook calls it only in the supplier_dues branch, nothing schedules it, and its only DB caller is fn_expense_post_to_gl (fires when a receipt arrives). A paid reveal therefore sits outside the GL — and outside the VAT-threshold watcher and the weekly report figures, both of which read posted 4xxx lines — until an unrelated receipt happens to arrive or the accountant's expense sweep runs.
  - evidence: stripe_webhook/index.ts L181 (supplier_dues branch only; no call in the quote_id branch L232-292); accountant/index.ts L850 comment 'Nothing calls fn_gl_sync_business on a schedule'; pg_proc callers → fn_expense_post_to_gl only; fn_accountant_vat_threshold_check reads gl_journal_lines account_code like '4%'
- **Degrades** — Three copies of the refund-chain constants and two routines with different interest bases. decision_policy (250 / 9%), pricing_config (250.00 / 0.0900) and gl_settings (250 / 0.75%/month) each hold the admin fee and rate. fn_refund_amount_aed charges 9% on the fee only; fn_supplier_nonresponse_penalty charges 9% on fee + AED 250. A supplier can be quoted two different totals for the same silence depending on which surface computes it.
  - evidence: pg_get_functiondef(fn_refund_amount_aed): v_interest := v_paid * v_pct/100 * days/365; pg_get_functiondef(fn_supplier_nonresponse_penalty): (v_recover + admin_fee) * apr * days/365; decision_policy, pricing_config, gl_settings rows
- **Degrades** — supplier_pay carries the open-redirect that was fixed in stripe_checkout on 27 Jul: return_url is taken from the request body with no host allowlist and becomes Stripe's success_url/cancel_url. A crafted call sends a paying supplier, with the Checkout Session id in the query string, to any origin.
  - evidence: supplier_pay/index.ts L98: const base = String(body.return_url \|\| 'https://quotesdone.com').replace(/\/+$/, ''); L101-102 use it; compare stripe_checkout/index.ts L49-68 safeBase()
- **Degrades · founder** — The test-mode proof path for refunds and invoices is half-configured: stripe_webhook_secret_test is present (3 Sep) but stripe_secret_key_test is absent from app_secrets, so a test-mode delivery verifies its signature and then logs 'INVOICE NOT RETRIEVED - no Stripe secret key for the test mode' / cannot look up a refund amount. The ten-scenario live Stripe test-mode run promised in docs/security/STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SETUP.md §D has no evidence in the estate (stripe_events 0 rows, both live and test).
  - evidence: app_secrets keys ilike '%stripe%' → stripe_secret_key, stripe_publishable_key, stripe_setup_token, stripe_webhook_secret, stripe_webhook_secret_test (no stripe_secret_key_test); stripe_webhook/index.ts L255, L284, L339-341; stripe_events count 0
- **Degrades** — Every finance cadence hangs on a single 15-minute Vercel cron hitting resilience_ping (VAT watcher, weekly report, OpenRouter meter, Dropbox mirror, outbox drain). pg_cron is empty. resilience_ping runs are already 28-40 s each. The only independent backstop is a once-a-day GitHub Actions ping.
  - evidence: vercel.json crons */15; cron.job 1 inactive row; agent_runs fn:resilience_ping notes 'slow run: 35036ms', '40224ms'; .github/workflows/resilience-ping.yml cron '17 7 * * *'
- **Degrades · founder** — The bank is not in the books and cannot be: no statement-import table or routine exists, GL 1000 Cash & Bank reads -7,464.61 because 52 expenses were credited against cash that was never debited in (no owner-capital or bank entry). The founder deferred the bank connection (instruction 21: PDF statement monthly, no API). Until a statement path exists the balance sheet cannot reconcile and the AED 200/month Mashreq charge is not booked.
  - evidence: information_schema search for %statement%/bank_% → 0 objects; gl_account_balances 1000 debit 187.85 credit 7,652.46; 3000 Owner Capital 0; founder_instruction 21 status deferred_by_founder
- **Degrades** — The accountant edge function itself has no scheduled entry point: 121 runs, all during the 9 Sep session, none since. Its SQL ticks run without it, and receipts reach the ledger via resend_inbound, so today nothing is lost — but the actions only it performs (expense_sweep across Gmail/Outlook, spend_report, refund_request routing, revenue_document signing) run only when a session invokes it.
  - evidence: agent_runs agent in ('accountant','fn:accountant') max(started_at) 2026-09-09 22:50; grep for functions/v1/accountant or '/accountant' outside its directory → 0; expense_entries created 10 Sep and 12 Sep with source 'receipt'
- **Cosmetic** — Two invoices per sale. stripe_checkout now has Stripe issue a real Invoice (captured and filed by the webhook), while reveal_invoice still renders its own 'QD-INV-xxxx' PDF via pdf_native with its own numbering and, once company_trn is set, its own 5%-inclusive VAT arithmetic. A customer and the accountant can hold two documents with two numbers for one payment.
  - evidence: stripe_checkout/index.ts L97-104; reveal_invoice/index.ts L46, L44-45, L51; both functions live and deployed; reveal_invoices 0 rows
- **Cosmetic · founder** — The OpenRouter/LLM hold binds once here: the accountant's 'assist' action and any model-written refund recommendation are held; refund routing (fn_agent_may_act → ceo_case_decision) and every money rule measured above are deterministic and unaffected. Noted, not raised.
  - evidence: accountant/index.ts L535 (assist); agent_runs accountant 'refused' ×2 on refund_request are deterministic refusals from fn_ceo_request_decision, not model failures

*Not measured:* Stripe account state (charges_enabled, payouts_enabled, balance, any charge or payout ever made on the live account): the Stripe MCP connector returned 'connection invalidated' and calling stripe_setup over HTTP is forbidden by the reader's rules — revenue = 0 is the ESTATE's record, not Stripe's.; Live webhook endpoint enabled_events (whether the four refund events were ever added to we_1TqVtRC45SqX5pVbEbnsquAL per docs/security/STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SETUP.md) — requires stripe_setup webhook_status or the Stripe API.; Whether a test-mode webhook endpoint exists in Stripe (only the test signing secret's presence in app_secrets was measured).; Stripe's actual UAE fee schedule; gl_settings 2.9% + AED 1.00 was used as the model without verification.; Whether transactions.quote_id carries a unique constraint (the two writers stripe_webhook/stripe_confirm rely on read-then-insert; stripe_confirm's error branch suggests one exists).; Whether the customer /me portal exposes a working path to stripe_checkout (founder said live portal tests are not information; UI not driven).; Dropbox folder contents (only doc_backups status rows and dropbox_backup run logs were read).; The W37 weekly report (due Mon 14 Sep ≥ 09:00 Dubai; measured at 00:41 Dubai).; Average quote value / average reveal fee — no quote has ever existed, so per-reveal figures are worked examples at AED 1k/10k/100k, not measurements.; Mashreq balance and the AED 200/month bank charge — no data path exists.; agent_runs before 2026-09-07 (retention boundary): any stripe_* run earlier than that is invisible.; vendor totals were summed in each entry's own currency; the AED-converted vendor ranking was not computed.

**Findings**

- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — Supply is zero and every request would match nobody: 0 of 15,305 supplier_capabilities rows have confirmed_at, 0 leads verified/claimed/signed, and the 171 'invited' leads have no delivery record (0 invite-type notifications). The two switches that let real invitations leave — invite_enabled and supplier_outreach_golive — are the founder's ('none of this will run till the app is perfect'). Downstream blocked: matching, quoting, comparison, reveal fee, every AED of revenue, the accountant's revenue paths (invoice capture, refunds, VAT threshold) which are proved only on shape. Proceeds without him: the portal-independent confirmation link the CEO report proposes can be built and proved against fixtures (ZZ DEMO / example.invalid); it cannot be sent to a real supplier until he flips the switch.
  - evidence: SQL: leads 22,264 / verified 0 / claimed 0 / signed_terms 0 / invited_at 171 (sent 161, bounced 10); notifications type ~ 'invite' = 0; supplier_capabilities confirmed_at 0 of 15,305; customers 0, inquiries 0, quotes 0, transactions 0; app_secrets invite_enabled='false', supplier_outreach_golive='false'; ceo_weekly_report.constraint_now names supply_confirmation as blocking.
- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — The supplier portal is parked by the founder until the website redesign, and it is the entry point of the only chain that writes supplier_capabilities.confirmed_at (capability_agent deleted 8 Sep; supplier_api capability_confirm serves the portal). The redesign itself is now instruction #41 (14 Sep) with no plan file yet. Downstream blocked: supplier self-declared scope, opt-in capture at registration (WhatsApp consent), signed T&C, the six mandatory verification fields. Proceeds without him: the confirmation-link route (CEO proposal), matching gates, the customer side, knowledge and archive work — all already shipped or independent.
  - evidence: Memory supplier-portal-waits-for-the-website-redesign (founder verbatim 9 Sep); founder_instruction #41 open; docs/audit contains no WEBSITE_AUDIT file at read time; ceo_weekly_report constraint_now proposal explicitly 'does not depend on the parked supplier portal'.
- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — The customer portal is declared incomplete by the founder (#36: 'the customer portal is not done … your tests will fail'), and it is the only door through which an inquiry is created; what specifically is incomplete is not recorded in the ledger and was not measured (UI not driven per his rule). Its drawing/photo intake is built but the reading/redaction arms are model-backed and held (finding 3). Proceeds without him: structural and unit-level proofs of the intake, per his own instruction #36.
  - evidence: founder_instruction #36 (done) verbatim; src/components/me/CustomerPortal.tsx intake path; inquiries 0, customers 0.
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY · founder** — The OpenRouter hold binds the three open sales_engineer instructions (#30 drawing→BOQ, #31 read any drawing format, #32 redact before sharing) and quote extraction: in the last 30 days 23 routings were HELD from drawing_boq, drawing_redact, quote_extract, resend_inbound and site_assistant with 0 provider calls. The deterministic parts (intake obligations, matching gates, field catalogue, retrieval on free gte-small embeddings, the CustomerPortal refusal to send an unredacted drawing) work without it; the model-backed reading/redaction cannot be exercised end to end. Spendable credit when it is lifted is USD 4.07 (key cap binds before the USD 14.07 balance). Noted once; not a recommendation.
  - evidence: SQL llm_route_health 8/8 open_until 2126-08-15; ai_cost_log 30d: 23 HELD / 0 called, fns listed; llm_attempt 30d: 0; v_openrouter_position read 13 Sep; ai_gateway/index.ts:484-505 fail-closed branch; founder_instruction #36 'the LLM is not open as well'.
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY · founder** — meta_access_token has no row, so fn_ads_autospend_state.live_allowed is false and the ad autopilot can only run in sandbox; meta_autospend_golive and meta_sync_enabled are also false. Only the founder can mint a system-user token — and per the 7 Sep reading, the ad account is personal and not inside the 'Quotes Done' business portfolio, so a business token would see zero ad accounts; moving it is irreversible and touches billing, hence his call. Downstream blocked: paid demand generation and Meta spend/insight sync. Proceeds without him: the decision engine, ledger and ceiling guard are proved in sandbox (rehearse_ad_spend.mjs 19/19 on 3 Sep, 26/26 on 4 Sep per docs).
  - evidence: SQL app_secrets key list (no meta_access_token; meta_autospend_golive 'false'; meta_sync_enabled 'false'; cap '30'); scripts/sql/ad_spend_needs_the_founders_cap_and_go.sql:45-69; company_policy_decision.meta_token_and_daily_cap; memory the-meta-business-portfolio-and-the-licence-facts (not re-measured).
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — Even with the token, nothing would call the ad autopilot: cron.job has 0 active jobs (pg_cron removed), resilience_ping's 11 ticks include no ads/meta tick, and agent_runs holds 0 rows for any meta/ads agent. The 'daily cron 316' the 3 Sep memory relies on no longer exists. This is engineering, not the founder. Boundary: GitHub Actions workflows were not checked for a scheduler.
  - evidence: SQL cron.job total 1 active 0; Read resilience_ping/index.ts:443-459 tick list; SQL agent_runs where agent ~* 'meta\|ads' = 0 rows.
- **Degrades · founder** — WhatsApp business-initiated messaging is gated by consent, not by Meta any more: 11 templates are approved (supplier_quote_accepted en+ar 6 Sep, founder_alert 9 Sep), wa_templates_ready=true, twilio_auth_token present — yet 0 of 22,264 leads carry wa_optin and outreach_channel_policy.whatsapp is blocked for exactly that reason. Opt-in capture lives at registration (supplier portal, parked) or in the invitation email (held by invite_enabled). The resubmitted '5 working days' supplier_quote_accepted pair sits at 'received' with Meta since 6 Sep — nothing for the founder to do; an approved earlier version exists. control_mode_code stays rejected (AUTHENTICATION category needs enabling in Meta Business Manager) but Control Mode is retired, so nothing waits on it. Proceeds without him: email copies of every notification (proved), the founder_alert template path.
  - evidence: SQL messaging_template 14 rows (statuses above); app_secrets wa_templates_ready='true', twilio_auth_token len 32 (7 Sep); leads wa_optin 0; notifications whatsapp all-time sent 1 / held 1; outreach_channel_policy.whatsapp reason text.
- **Degrades · founder** — stripe_secret_key_test is absent, so the platform cannot run its own sandbox checkout rehearsal and a test-mode refund whose amount is missing from Stripe's event is quarantined rather than looked up (stripe_webhook falls back to ''). Nothing is blocked for real customers: live keys and the live webhook are in place. Proceeds without him: every live-mode path; the manual Payment-Link rehearsal (5/5 on 3 Sep).
  - evidence: SQL app_secrets: no stripe_secret_key_test row; stripe_secret_key prefix sk_live_; stripe_webhook_secret_test present (3 Sep); supabase/functions/stripe_webhook/index.ts:255,284,340; docs/FOUNDER_INPUTS.md item 5; transactions 0.
- **Degrades · founder** — Bank reconciliation depends on a monthly Mashreq statement PDF the founder will share once operating, and no importer exists to receive it (0 routines). AED 200/month charges (two debited) are deliberately unbooked by his instruction. Downstream: the accountant's cash position is a book balance, never a confirmed bank balance; Stripe settlements and bank charges will arrive on one statement. Proceeds without him: a PDF statement importer with the 6300 G&A mapping can be built and proved on a synthetic statement; it cannot be proved on a real one.
  - evidence: founder_instruction #21 deferred_by_founder; grep found no importer; memory the-bank-is-mashreq-and-statements-come-monthly; the_accountant_writes_a_weekly_financial_report…sql:392 labels the figure 'a book balance, not a confirmed bank statement'.
- **Degrades · founder** — Four accounting inputs only the founder holds: OpenAI/ChatGPT monthly charges (portal_only — Aug/Sep real and unbooked, he said he will share), the GoDaddy possible double charge (only his GoDaddy account can settle it), the missing Vercel invoice OOJ7APIU-0002, and OpenRouter recharge receipts to the expenses mailbox. Downstream: the books (AED 6,905.59 at 9 Sep) understate spend by the unbooked OpenAI months. Proceeds without him: everything else in the ledger.
  - evidence: SQL vendor_billing_ended OpenAI kind=portal_only; memory checkpoint-9-sep-the-accountant-and-the-books; company_policy_decision.openrouter_credit.
- **Cosmetic** — docs/FOUNDER_INPUTS.md (4 Sep) is stale on two of six items: item 2 says the cap 'today holds UNSET' (live value '30' since 6 Sep, his number) and item 4 still waits on a template Meta has since approved. An auditor reading it would send him to set a value he already set.
  - evidence: Read of docs/FOUNDER_INPUTS.md vs SQL app_secrets.meta_daily_budget_cap_aed='30' (updated 2026-09-06) and messaging_template supplier_quote_accepted approved 2026-09-06.
- **Cosmetic** — Two open ledger rows (#34 code organisation, #35 decisions must land in the ledger) are standing rules that were implemented (the skill exists, PR #565) but never closed with evidence, and #39 (knowledge to senior-expert reasoning) is explicitly 'work it out later'. An index that says open for a rule already in force is the failure #35 describes.
  - evidence: SQL founder_instruction ids 34, 35, 39 status open, evidence NULL; .claude/CLAUDE.md lists the skill; commit 7f94221.
- **Cosmetic · founder** — One founder-held fact remains open from the knowledge plan: UBO filing status (plan §5 row 3 residue). Everything else in §5 (licence, VAT, CT, auditor, Stripe facts, Meta cap, refund ladder, sanctions, discounts, category threshold, budgets, supplier pricing, standing rules) is closed and marked never_ask_again.
  - evidence: docs/KNOWLEDGE_PLAN_2026-09-06.md:1162; company_policy_decision 14 rows never_ask_again=true.

**Findings**

- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — No supplier can be matched or can quote: the two supply-side gates are both at zero. fn_match_capability requires supplier_capabilities.confirmed_at (0 of 15,305) and fn_is_matchable requires leads.verified (0 of 22,264; approval_status NULL on all). submit_quote additionally requires claimed_by, accepted_terms_at and approval_status='approved' (0, 0, 0). A real customer tomorrow produces an inquiry that reaches nobody and can never receive a quote, so no reveal, no 3% fee.
  - evidence: Counts measured 2026-09-14 (see measurements). customer_api:207-224: when sales_engineer answers ok with matched=[], routerAnswered=true and the keyword fallback is suppressed; :256 flagCoverageGap fires (founder_alerts high + admin notification + agent_actions) and returns matches:0. Even the keyword path (match_suppliers_kw) excludes unapproved suppliers per supplier_api:531-533 comment. supplier_api:823-826 refuses submit_quote unless approval_status='approved'.
- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — Under the LLM hold the intake cannot mark a classified request 'ready', so create_inquiry answers 428 intake_required for every request in a recognised category. The deterministic fallback returns needs_info with brief=null whenever the detected category has required fields, and 56 of 56 categories do. Only text that no classifier recognises can pass (category null → checklist empty → 'ready' with category null → filed as 'general').
  - evidence: inquiry_intake/index.ts:387-395 (missingReq from checklist.required; brief null on needs_info); :470-491 readiness gate on required keys; customer_api:728-735 requires intake.status==='ready'. category_specs with_required = 56/56 measured. llm_route_health 8/8 held. This is where the founder's OpenRouter hold binds on the loop (also drawing_boq: no fallback; quote_extract: upload quotes fail; quote_compare: deterministic fallback exists). Code read, not executed.
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — The refund ladder has no scheduler. fn_refund_ladder_sweep (day-3 chase email + customer_support task, day-5 refund filing + final-call alert) is called by nothing: pg_cron has 0 active jobs, resilience_ping's 15-minute tick runs 11 other rpcs and not this one, no GitHub or Vercel cron names it. fn_refund_state can therefore never reach 'chased' or 'final_call_sent', refund_approvable stays true only by clock, and the customer-facing promise in trg_reveal_receipt ('we chase them for you… decided by our chief executive') cannot be kept.
  - evidence: grep fn_refund_ladder_sweep over supabase/functions, .github, vercel.json = 0; cron.job total 1 active 0; resilience_ping rpc list measured; fn_refund_ladder_sweep and fn_refund_state sources read.
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY · founder** — Moving refund money requires a human Control Mode session and a founder/admin identity: refunds_api 'resolve' is gated by fn_control_mode_require and there are 0 open sessions (the only one expired 2026-08-04). No agent seat can execute a refund; the CEO seat's 'decide' only writes ceo_approval and links refunds.refund_decision_id, and nothing invokes ceo_agent when a refund is requested (customer_api's fn_refund_open_from_customer creates no ceo_task, unlike fn_refund_request).
  - evidence: refunds_api/index.ts:130-151; control_mode_session open_now=0 measured; ceo_agent/index.ts:250-283 read; fn_refund_open_from_customer source read (inserts refunds only); ceo_task=2 open rows unrelated; grep for ceo_agent invokers in .github/vercel.json/src = 0. Contradicts founder ruling 8 Sep (all approvals are the CEO's; nothing goes to the founder) and #1 of 9 Sep (accountant issues the refund) — accountant/index.ts:20 says it 'does not execute a refund (refunds_api does)'.
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — Three refund representations, two of which have no runtime writer: (1) transactions.refund_status + public.refunds (written by customer_api/refund_flow/refunds_api/webhook); (2) refund_case + supplier_response_watch + ceo_approval-by-fn_ceo_decide_refund + fn_refund_execute_authorized — no edge function calls fn_support_investigate_refund, fn_support_record_supplier_contact/response, fn_ceo_decide_refund or fn_refund_execute_authorized, and fn_refund_execute_authorized never calls Stripe (it records an outcome the caller asserts). fn_notify_supplier (the live 'notify' path) does not write supplier_response_watch, so fn_refund_nonresponse_eligibility always answers 'no_watch' and z10 makes a non-response approval on refund_case impossible.
  - evidence: grep of supabase/functions for each routine name = 0; sources of fn_support_investigate_refund, fn_support_record_supplier_contact, fn_refund_execute_authorized, fn_refund_nonresponse_eligibility, fn_notify_supplier read; refund_case=0, supplier_response_watch=0. founder_instruction #1 (9 Sep) evidence names this chain as applied.
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — customer_api.request_refund records 'requested' and opens a refunds row but routes it to nobody: no ceo_task, no refund_case, no supplier_response_watch, no customer_support task. The response tells the customer 'we check whether the supplier ever replied… our chief executive decides' — a status column, not a delivery record.
  - evidence: customer_api:1053-1090 and fn_refund_open_from_customer source (single insert into refunds, returns prose 'next'); trg_emit_refund_requested emits company_event 'refund.requested' whose consumer was not identified in this pass (workflows removed 7 Sep per memory).
- **Degrades** — If a refund is issued via refunds_api before the CEO seat has linked a decision, Stripe's refund.* webhook cannot be recorded: fn_refund_settle matches the customer's 'requested' refunds row by payment_intent and updates status to succeeded, and z05_refund_execution_requires_ceo_approval raises because requested_by='customer' and refund_decision_id is NULL. Money moves at Stripe, the ledger refuses the row, stripe_webhook logs 'REFUND NOT RECORDED'.
  - evidence: fn_refund_settle source (matches status in approved/processing/requested, then UPDATE status); fn_refund_execution_requires_ceo_approval source (exempts only stripe_webhook/stripe_dashboard/stripe as requested_by); refund_status_transition requested→succeeded allowed=true; stripe_webhook:368-370. Code read, not executed; refunds=0 so never observed.
- **Degrades · founder** — refunds_api approve expects a database trigger to raise the supplier's 3% non-response due and block matching; that trigger (qd_on_refund_resolved) is attached to no table, so every approval will escalate 'Refund approved but supplier was not charged' to founder_alerts and admin email, and supplier_dues stays empty — a guard that fires when the system works.
  - evidence: refunds_api/index.ts:193, :211-221; pg_trigger check: qd_on_refund_resolved attached=false; supplier_dues=0; triggers on transactions enumerated (4, none touching supplier_dues).
- **Degrades** — Reveal confirmation can email the supplier twice: stripe_confirm (browser path) inserts a 'quote_accepted' notification itself (:59) and the transactions insert it performs also fires z20_reveal_receipt, which inserts a second 'quote_accepted' to the same supplier. stripe_webhook removed its own insert on 3 Sep and relies on the trigger; stripe_confirm did not.
  - evidence: stripe_confirm/index.ts:57-59; trg_reveal_receipt source part 2; stripe_webhook/index.ts:263-266 comment. Code read; transactions=0 so never observed.
- **Degrades · founder** — Stripe test-mode reveals cannot capture their invoice: the webhook accepts test-signed events (stripe_webhook_secret_test present) but stripe_secret_key_test is absent from app_secrets, so captureInvoice is skipped with 'no Stripe secret key for the test mode' and refund-amount lookups in test mode fall back to quarantine.
  - evidence: app_secrets key query: stripe_secret_key_test not returned; stripe_webhook:255-257, :339-341.
- **Degrades · founder** — Both the reveal texts and the ladder still route the final call to the founder: stripe_confirm's supplier email says 'our founder makes a final call'; fn_notify_supplier says 'the case goes to our founder'; fn_refund_ladder_sweep calls fn_notify_founder(p_kind='refund_final_call') and its customer message in request_refund says 'the case has gone to our founder'. The 8 Sep ruling moved every approval to the CEO seat and the webhook-side receipt (trg_reveal_receipt) already says 'chief executive'.
  - evidence: stripe_confirm/index.ts:59; fn_notify_supplier and fn_refund_ladder_sweep sources; customer_api:1060. One surface (trg_reveal_receipt) was updated 6 Sep, the others were not.
- **Degrades** — A 9 Sep reveal rehearsal reached a real supplier: the only quote_accepted notification ever sent went to lead 27579 (MARINA OCEAN BUILDING MATERIALS L.L.C, real domain, unclaimed, never invited) via the outbox, while the fixture transaction was later deleted. The fixture convention (ZZ DEMO / @example.invalid) was not applied to the supplier side of that reveal.
  - evidence: notifications row type quote_accepted status sent created 2026-09-09 20:08:43 lead_id 27579; leads row: is_demo false, demo_email false, claimed false, invited_at null; transactions=0.
- **Degrades** — Two finished capabilities have no entry point from the customer portal: reveal_invoice (platform receipt PDF) and refund_flow (state/notify) are not referenced by src/components/me/CustomerPortal.tsx, so a paying customer cannot download the receipt or press 'Notify supplier' from the surface that exists.
  - evidence: grep counts on CustomerPortal.tsx: reveal_invoice 0, refund_flow 0 (customer_api 29, stripe_checkout 1, stripe_confirm 1). Portal is known-incomplete per the founder; not driven.

*Not measured:* Whether a Stripe webhook endpoint is registered at Stripe and pointed at /functions/v1/stripe_webhook, and whether any live or test event was ever delivered before the 7 Sep agent_runs horizon — no Stripe API call was made (read-only rule; Stripe MCP not exercised).; Row history before 2026-09-07 18:15 (agent_runs horizon) — the founder ordered old history deleted on 9 Sep, so 'never exercised' means 'no record since the horizon'.; Whether the /me portal's kyc flow actually sets full_name, phone and tos_accepted_at so create_inquiry's 428 gate can be passed — portal not driven per founder instruction.; The writer of public.quote_comparisons (quote_compare writes qd_compare_cache) and the consumer of company_event 'refund.requested'.; drawing_redact's redaction quality/model dependency and cad_convert (CloudConvert) reachability — not read.; Whether match_suppliers_kw would return any supplier if approval_status were set — not executed against 22,264 leads.; End-to-end behaviour of stripe_webhook / fn_refund_settle / z05 interaction — inferred from source, not executed (refunds=0, transactions=0).; The 11 supplier_api HTTP 200 calls on 13 Sep 20:38-20:45 — which actions they were (notes empty) and who made them.; Whether fn_notify_founder's founder_mail_policy would deliver the refund_final_call page if the sweep ever ran.

**Findings**

- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — Every seat's 'reasoning' is retrieve-and-quote. No seat fuses, compares or derives: sales_engineer and accountant concatenate cited passages verbatim; ceo_context returns records plus a signal string; the only surface that composes an answer is the public widget via ai_gateway. This is where the OpenRouter hold binds, noted once: with it in place a seat can find a passage but cannot apply a diagnostic lens, a decision card or a price band to a case. Not a recommendation to lift it.
  - evidence: sales_engineer/index.ts:179-184 (reply = records joined); accountant/index.ts:559-565; fn_ceo_context returns 'records' + 'signal'; site_assistant/index.ts:297 gateway call
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — The CEO seat is the thinnest relative to its bar: 508 lexical-only records, 96 synthetic, 163 requiring current verification, all 508 'authored_not_independently_reviewed'; 72 metric definitions with no threshold or benchmark; 40 decision cards all enabled=false and 'not executable'; 0 logged runs of the context action; 3 agent_knowledge rows; exam results 0. It cannot see kb_chunks, ops_knowledge or the other seats' packs from any deployed path.
  - evidence: ceo_knowledge counts and flags; fn_ceo_knowledge_search reads ceo_knowledge only; agent_runs has no 'ceo' rows; knowledge_exam_results 0 rows
- **Degrades** — The declared knowledge scopes are decoration: org_role.knowledge_scopes is read by nothing (0 routines, 0 views, 0 edge functions), and 10 of the CEO's 18 scopes name kb_chunks categories that hold 0 rows. What a seat can actually retrieve is decided inside each assist routine, not by the scope list.
  - evidence: pg_proc/pg_views/grep = 0 consumers; scope->category_norm join: communication, company, company_goals, constitution, evolution, legal_uae, priority_hierarchy, quotesdone_os, sales_crm, universal_objectives = 0 chunks each
- **Degrades** — customer_support's 408-record pack, its 40 decision playbooks and its 8 pinned invariants reach no deployed surface. v_support_corpus forces is_public to FAQ rows only (a deliberate control after a draft WhatsApp template leaked to a visitor), site_assistant is the only caller and passes p_public=true, and fn_support_context -- the internal path that would carry the pack and the invariants -- has no caller. The support seat a customer talks to answers from 175 public FAQ rows plus price bands.
  - evidence: pg_get_viewdef(v_support_corpus): `is_public AND src='faq' AND ...`; v_support_corpus supportkb 408 rows / 0 public, ops 9,139 / 0 public; grep fn_support_context in supabase/functions = 0; site_assistant/index.ts:243
- **Degrades** — The sales engineer refuses its own knowledge on natural phrasings. 4 of the 5 open gaps are 'what do I need to tell you to get a quote for X' questions; sales_knowledge holds the sector_guide for each (structural steel, fire alarm, HVAC chiller, LV panel) and an OR-of-lexemes query ranks it at 0.16-0.38, but fn_sales_assist admits an embedded row only if the ALL-terms websearch_to_tsquery matches (0 rows for each question). The accountant/IT routines were given a 60%-overlap alternative for exactly this measured failure ('9 of 35 real questions at ts_rank 0.0000'); the sales routine deliberately withholds it after one false positive.
  - evidence: sales_knowledge_gap open rows: best_similarity 0.889-0.916, best_lexical_rank 0.0000; SQL all_terms=0 vs any_term 327-1,149 for each; fn_sales_assist header comment on the reverted overlap rule
- **Degrades** — The IT engineer's knowledge is the best-shaped corpus for senior reasoning (16 diagnostic lenses, 14 failure signatures, structured lens/symptom/monitor_rule keys) and has never been exercised: the assist action has 0 runs, and the edge function passes no embedding, so the vector route the routine was designed around is dead unless an external caller computes the vector itself.
  - evidence: agent_runs it_engineer: authority 6, health 4, status 2, assist 0; it_engineer/index.ts:322-325 p_embedding = body.embedding ?? null; fn_it_engineer_assist floor 0.83 only applies when p_embedding is not null
- **Degrades** — The company operating body (ops_knowledge, 9,139 rows, 24 MB, 100% embedded, 270 decision playbooks, 246 SOPs, 692 failure-mode cases) is scoped by a department layer that was removed on 7 Sep: org_agents.department is NULL for all five seats, so fn_ops_knowledge_match returns nothing for any live seat and fn_ops_knowledge_search has no dept to scope by. The three assist routines reach it only by reading it unscoped (top-20 by cosine), and ceo/customer_support do not reach it at all.
  - evidence: select department from org_agents for the five keys -> null; fn_ops_knowledge_match body: `if v_dept is null then return`; v_support_corpus ops 0 public
- **Degrades** — Structured qualification knowledge exists in four disconnected shapes with no typed answers or ranges: category_specs 396 fields (typed: select/multiselect/number/text), qualification_playbooks 282 questions (typed choice), sales_knowledge 896 qualification_field rows (one answer_type for all, zero marked critical, no options, no ranges), and 1,627 unembedded 'cat:' agent_knowledge rows with the most concrete intake content (mm2 cable sizes, kA ratings). A senior sales engineer holds one model per trade of which answers gate a quote and what typical values are; the estate holds four lists that do not reference each other.
  - evidence: category_specs field type counts; qualification_playbooks 29/282; sales_knowledge distinct answer_type = 1, criticality 'critical' = 0; agent_knowledge cat:% 1,627 rows / 0 embedded
- **Degrades** — The accountant corpus deliberately holds no rate, threshold or deadline (obligation_shape_no_figures 15, 'resolve_from_the_fta' on 9 records). That is a correct epistemic policy for a corpus without a feed, but it means the seat cannot compute a VAT position, a return or a penalty exposure -- the figures a senior accountant carries in their head are absent by design, and there is no table that holds them either (company_registration carries only the 375,000 threshold and the not-registered flag).
  - evidence: epistemic_status counts; fn_accountant_assist handoff text; fn_company_context section 4
- **Degrades** — 6.8% of the library is embedded, and the embedded share is concentrated away from what the seats need: standard 1.1%, reference 0%, cert 0.2%, book 0%; the seat scopes sit at 2.8-37.3%. Vector retrieval over kb_chunks therefore sees a sample, and the lexical route inside fn_sales_assist ranks only the first 4,000 index hits (marked ponytail:), so recall on common terms is bounded by index order, not relevance.
  - evidence: kb_chunks by source_kind; scope table; fn_sales_assist c_kb_scan := 4000
- **Degrades** — No instrument measures whether any seat's knowledge is used or correct: kb_usage_log 0 rows, knowledge_kpis.queries_30d 0, knowledge_exam_results 0 rows, exam questions absent for 4 of 5 seats, v_knowledge_compliance empty for the 5 seats, all assist runs dated 9 Sep (build day) and none since. 'Built and proven to answer 35 of 35' (accountant/index.ts:526-527) is a code comment, not a stored result.
  - evidence: direct counts; agent_runs max(started_at) = 2026-09-09 for every seat
- **Cosmetic** — Compliance checklists were miscounted as zero. 21 of 56 categories carry a compliance_checklist (81 items) and 21 carry supplier_requirements (74 items); both are stored as {"list":[...]} objects, and an array-length count returns 0. The real gap is the other 35 categories (including hvac, electrical, plumbing, mep) which carry neither.
  - evidence: jsonb_typeof(compliance_checklist->'list')='array' count = 21; sum = 81; sample security row lists SIRA/TDRA items

*Not measured:* Retrieval precision/recall of fn_accountant_assist and fn_it_engineer_assist against a held-out question set (the '35 of 35' claim lives only in a code comment; knowledge_exam_results is empty, and calling the assist routines would write agent_runs / sales_decision_log / org_tasks, which is not read-only); Content quality of kb_chunks (2.6M rows) -- only counts, sizes and embedding share were measured; Whether the 1,627 'cat:' agent_knowledge rows cover the 56 category_specs trades (132 category keys vs 56 slugs was not joined); fn_price_guidance definition and its refusal behaviour (site_assistant reads it; the routine body was not dumped); wa_inbound / customer_api retrieval paths (grep shows ai_gateway try_faq only; not read line by line); dept_knowledge_alias and org_departments.merged_into contents (fn_dept_scope inputs) -- only the null-department consequence was measured; The effect of the 4,000-row lexical scan cap on recall (would need a probe that writes); MB figures use length(content) in characters; byte size differs for Arabic content; Support and CEO retrieval quality on Arabic queries beyond the corpus routine's tsquery branches; Portal UI behaviour (founder ruled live tests are not information); Whether ai_gateway try_faq level-0 answers are logged anywhere the seats can learn from (kb_usage_log is empty; ai_gateway logs not read)

**Findings**

- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — FIRST STUCK STEP, CUSTOMER (EN and AR): the deterministic intake cannot reach 'ready' for any recognisable job. inquiry_intake's no-model branch rebuilds `missingReq` from the category's required checklist on every turn and never subtracts what the customer answered, and the 'one definition of ready' gate then demands canonical keys the fallback never fills. All 56 categories carry required fields, so a request that names a trade (English keyword map, or fn_kb_route_decide for Arabic) is held at needs_info indefinitely and 'Send to suppliers' never appears; create_inquiry independently refuses without a ready intake row (428 intake_required). Only text that resolves to no category slips through and is filed as 'general'.
  - evidence: inquiry_intake/index.ts 387-395 (missingReq computed from checklist, not from answers) and 470-491 (ready gate); category_specs 56/56 with required fields (measured); llm_route_health 8/8 open; inquiry_intake table 0 rows. Boundary: derived from code + data, not by executing an intake against production.
- **BLOCKS REVENUE** — Even a request that gets through reaches nobody: 0 suppliers are matchable. fn_match_capability requires supplier_capabilities.confirmed_at and 0 of 15,305 rows have it; the only writer (capability_agent) is not deployed and not in the repo, so supplier_api's capability_confirm forwards to a 404 and no supplier can ever clear the 'Confirm what your company can quote for' blocking item. Keyword fallback also returns 0 because 0 leads are claimed/approved.
  - evidence: fn_match_capability(hvac, Dubai)=0, (painting, any)=0, match_suppliers_kw=0 (measured); supplier_api 281-320 fetches functions/v1/capability_agent; list_edge_functions (65) has no such slug; supabase/functions has no such directory; leads claimed 0, approval_status null on all 22,264.
- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — FIRST STUCK STEP, SUPPLIER (EN and AR): a self-registering supplier cannot complete the registration form. With licence extraction returning nothing (license_extract depends on ai_gateway, all routes open), ProfileForm sets licNote to 'Licence uploaded.', which hides the manual licence-number inputs, and save() then refuses 'Add your trade licence number'. Reload keeps them hidden because license_doc_path was already stored. The server-side gate would have approved on the stored path alone; the browser never gets there.
  - evidence: SupplierPortal.tsx 1108-1118 and 1178 ((isEdit \|\| !licNote) guard; save() requires f.trade_license_no); supplier_api process_docs 351-363 returns extracted:{} with license_error; license_extract 92-106 returns 502 ai_error on a non-ok gateway reply; ai_gateway 502 returns outcome circuit_open. Boundary: read from code; no registration executed.
- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — Supplier acquisition is switched off: invitations are the only supplier entry path that scales and both gates (supplier_outreach_golive, invite_enabled) are false, so every daily batch since 3 Sep is planned 50 / sent 0. Historically 171 invites produced 0 claims. The invitation text and link are English only (/en/portal?t=...).
  - evidence: app_secrets values; invite_schedule rows 2026-09-03..07 'HELD'; leads invite_status sent 161 / bounced 10 / claimed 0; invite_body has no Arabic characters; enqueue_supplier_invites builds link with '/en/portal?t='.
- **BLOCKS REVENUE · founder** — The OpenRouter hold binds these customer/supplier steps with no deterministic substitute: drawing analysis and redaction (so no drawing ever reaches a supplier), voice transcription, trade-licence extraction, supplier quotation-PDF extraction, and the intake's question-asking. Deterministic substitutes DO exist for: FAQ/price answers in the widget, quote comparison (deterministicCompare), the quote form path, and the licence gate itself. Noted once; not a recommendation to lift it.
  - evidence: functions posting to ai_gateway with no non-model branch: drawing_boq 57-63, drawing_redact 258-262, transcribe, license_extract 92-106, quote_extract; quote_compare 208 has deterministicCompare; site_assistant 405-493 composes price/gap/corpus answers without a model.
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — The production customer side is empty: 0 customers, 0 inquiries, 0 quotes, 0 transactions, 0 refunds, 0 intake drafts, while 17 auth.users exist and 2 customer sign-in codes were used in the last 30 days. Every /me endpoint answers 403 'No customer profile' to an auth user without a customers row; the email OTP verify path re-inserts the row on the next sign-in, the mobile path cannot (no row to resolve the number to).
  - evidence: SQL counts; login_otp 267-272 (insert on verify); customer_api 599-600, stripe_checkout 31-32, stripe_confirm 29-30, refund_flow 34-35 (403 on missing row); resolveEmailByPhone reads customers.phone.
- **Degrades** — Arabic is second-class on every transactional surface. Customer portal: the whole New-request intake UI (greeting, placeholders, brief card, 'Send to suppliers', multi-trade choice), the reveal row ('Reveal & contact', fee sentence), Contact actions (rate/hired/refund) and all confirm() dialogs, Edit/Cancel request labels, and the emirate list are English-only strings; deterministic intake questions are the English category_specs questions. Supplier portal: NewCompany trade placeholder and hint, the 'Scope unclear?' prompt/alert, and every server-composed eligibility/alert sentence are English only. All emails (sign-in code, email-verify code, invitation, new_inquiry, registration_received, new_quote, quote_accepted, job_awarded_other) are English only and none reads leads.locale/customers.locale. The website, widget shell, portal shells, auth screens, comparison card and quote spread ARE bilingual.
  - evidence: CustomerPortal.tsx NewRequest() 1351-1513 (hard-coded English), QuoteCard 1313-1314, Contact 1324-1345, InquiryCard 1027, 1081-1082; SupplierPortal.tsx 1078-1080, 754; supplier_portal eligibility 498-561; notification bodies in customer_api 123-124, 270, supplier_api 614, 921, stripe_confirm 59; login_otp wrapCode; kyc wrapCode; app_secrets invite_body has no Arabic.
- **Degrades** — The widget's Arabic fallback chain is shorter than the English one: the support-corpus fallback tokenises with /[^a-z0-9]+/, so an Arabic question can never share a word with a corpus hit and always drops to the knowledge-gap reply. Measured 9 Arabic turns, 4 answered (both answered ones were price/gap composers). Also observed in the log: on 2026-09-09 21:15Z two non-price English questions were answered with a gypsum price band; a minute later the same questions were answered correctly (cause not established from the rows).
  - evidence: site_assistant/index.ts 482-493; assistant_logs sample rows 2026-09-09 21:15:08-21:16:26.
- **Degrades** — CustomerPortal mounts refund through customer_api request_refund; the separate refund_flow function ('Notify supplier' after 1 working day, list of refund states) is deployed but has no caller in the portal, so the customer sees only 'Request refund' and its confirm() text, and never the ladder state. What runs the 3-working-day chase and the day-5 decision on a schedule was not established (pg_cron has 0 active jobs; resilience_ping's tick was read for send_outbox only).
  - evidence: CustomerPortal.tsx Contact() 1324 calls customer_api request_refund only; grep of src for refund_flow: no caller; cron.job active 0.
- **Cosmetic** — The login_otp 'turned away 50%' watcher measures a real refusal ratio but of a surface that logs only refusals plus observe() twins over a 24h window of ~24 callers, all on 7-9 Sep; both refusal kinds are by design (phone sign-up refused, shared number refused) and the customers table those callers matched against is now empty. The finding is recorded as resolved/closed. It is not evidence that email sign-in fails (2 of 2 email codes used).
  - evidence: it_watch_finding refusal_rate:login_otp evidence.basis and not_covered; agent_runs distribution by hour; email_verifications 2/2.

*Not measured:* Live behaviour of either portal in a browser (per the founder's standing rule); every 'works/broken' above is read from code and production rows, not driven.; Whether the 128 login_otp refusals on 7-9 Sep were real people or a probe/rehearsal; the rows do not carry enough to tell.; What schedules enqueue_supplier_invites, the supplier chase at 3 working days, the refund sweep at day 5, and the 14-day licence exception clock now that pg_cron has 0 active jobs (only the send_outbox call inside resilience_ping was read).; Whether fn_founder_brief or fn_sales_portal_report write supplier_capabilities.confirmed_at (their source matched a text filter; not read line by line).; Actual Resend/Twilio/Stripe delivery outcomes for a real customer (keys are present; no customer-facing message has been queued in 60 days).; WhatsApp channel (wa_inbound) for customers and suppliers — out of this dimension's scope and not read.; Bounce/spam state of the 17 auth.users mailboxes (email_suppressions not joined).; The supplier portal's 20 secondary tabs beyond registration, inbox, quote, documents, messages and billing — mounted, not read.; messages_api chat gating (request_chat after a paid reveal) — deployed, not read.

| OTP handling | built and exercised | Email codes stored as HMAC (app_secrets.otp_hmac_key, comment on rainbow-table defence); mobile OTP delegated to Twilio Verify with a ledger row and no code stored. 17 auth.users, 6 QA/fixture; user_roles: 2 owner rows (management@quotesdone.com, ahmadalzain1993@admin.quotesdone.com), RLS on, service_role only. — `supabase/functions/login_otp/index.ts, mobile_otp/index.ts` |
| Post-reveal messaging privacy | built and exercised | thread/request_chat return locked until a transactions row exists for the pair; blocked messages excluded; auto-ban on off-platform contact pushing (customers.banned read by customer_api MUTATING list). — `supabase/functions/messages_api/index.ts` |
| Cross-supplier portal routines | built and exercised | prosrc contains no company_name and no email/phone output; aggregates by sector/emirate centroid. fn_supplier_workspaces scoped to p_lead_id via inquiry_matches/messages. — `fn_supplier_market, fn_supplier_network_map, fn_supplier_workspaces (SECURITY DEFINER, service_role only)` |
