**Findings**

- **Degrades** — A probe fired the real reveal receipt at a real supplier on 9 Sep and nothing structural stops the class: the only outbound containment (zzz_notifications_demo_guard) is a switch that defaults to off, so any transactions/notifications row a probe writes against a real leads.id emails that company.
  - evidence: notifications 21354 to i***@marinatrans.com (lead 27579), type quote_accepted, body 'A customer paid to reveal your contact for: .', status sent, inquiry_id NULL, quote_id NULL, 2026-09-09 20:08 UTC; transactions for supplier 27579 today = 0; decision_policy.demo_rehearsal_active = false; guard prosrc returns NEW unchanged when the switch is false. No founder_instruction, agent_runs or commit text mentions 27579/marinatrans, so the incident is unrecorded. Boundary: I found no fix file for this; if one exists it is not named after the event.
- **Degrades** — supplier_api 'search' returns claimed_by (the claiming account's email) for every matching lead to any signed-in supplier — a cross-supplier identity disclosure of the same class as the role_guard membership oracle fixed on 19 Jul.
  - evidence: Deployed supplier_api version 9 (get_edge_function) line: select('id,company_name,emirate,business_role,claimed,claimed_by') ... return J({ results: (data\|\|[]).map((r)=>({...r, claimable: ...})) }). Repo supplier_api/index.ts:255 identical. Impact today is nil because leads.claimed = 0 rows; it becomes live with the first claimed supplier.
- **Degrades** — Credential rotation does not exist as a running mechanism: the pg_cron scheduler was emptied on 7 Sep (1 inactive job remains) and no surviving scheduler (Vercel cron, 3 GitHub workflows) rotates anything; 40 of 41 internal credentials have never rotated and 9 active ones have never been presented.
  - evidence: cron.job count 1 (wa_flush, active=false); cron.job_run_details max(start_time) 2026-09-07 17:51; v_internal_credential_health rotated_at NULL on 40/41, use_count 0 on 9 active; vercel.json crons = resilience-ping only; .github/workflows schedules = resilience-ping, kb-embed-backfill, project-parity. The memory note 'Credentials rotate themselves — cron 334' no longer describes production.
- **Degrades** — Long-lived third-party secrets sit in plaintext in public.app_secrets with no rotation since issue: OpenRouter key (98 days), Stripe live secret and webhook secret (76 days), Resend API key (96 days), GitHub token (52 days), Vercel token (87 days), Dropbox refresh token (73 days), plus a QA customer password. Protection is one layer: RLS with no policy and no anon/authenticated grant, read only through the service-role key.
  - evidence: app_secrets inventory by key/length/prefix/updated_at (values not read). Twilio auth token is the only provider secret rotated recently (2026-09-07). Any service-role compromise (edge function env, CI token) exposes all 214 keys at once.
- **Degrades** — contact_submit is unauthenticated with no rate limit beyond a honeypot; each accepted POST inserts a contact_messages row and sends an email to admin_emails[0] through Resend directly, bypassing the notifications choke point and the demo guard.
  - evidence: supabase/functions/contact_submit/index.ts: no rl_check, no IP cap; direct fetch to api.resend.com; demo_rehearsal_containment.sql explicitly lists contact_submit among four direct senders the trigger cannot see.
- **Cosmetic** — Public 'site' and 'brand' buckets still serve retired admin/OS/CRM consoles (site/admin/index.html, site/os/app.html, brand/crm.html, brand/admin_index.html) after the OS was deleted on 9 Sep; they carry only the anon key, so this is an attack-surface and confusion issue, not a key leak.
  - evidence: storage.objects listing of the 4 public buckets; HTTP GET of each HTML: service_role markers 0, anon JWT 1 each.
- **Cosmetic** — The 495 rls_enabled_no_policy INFO advisories and the 43 mutable search_path WARNs are noise for exposure purposes: the affected tables have no anon/authenticated grants and none of the 43 routines is SECURITY DEFINER. The 2 authenticated SECDEF warnings are correctly scoped functions and the 3 extension_in_public warnings are inert.
  - evidence: Measured as listed under measurements: anon selects denied on leads/inquiries/inquiries_supplier_safe; prosecdef false for all 43; fn_customer_document_refs/fn_customer_documents filter on auth.email()/auth.uid().
- **Cosmetic · founder** — OpenRouter hold binds here once: every masking layer that matters (scrub_inquiry_contacts, scrubIdentity, the support-corpus source rule) is deterministic, so the privacy posture does not depend on the model. The one model-dependent scrub is quote_extract's upload path, which supplier_api deliberately does not re-scrub.
  - evidence: supplier_api submit_quote: scrub applies only when source !== 'upload'; comment states quote_extract's prompt anonymises the upload path.

*Not measured:* Whether the deployed copies of customer_api, supplier_portal and the other 62 functions are byte-identical to the repository (only supplier_api was fetched from production and compared on the checked lines).; Whether any edge function returns an app_secrets value to a client response (secretVal/getSecret call sites were not traced to every J() return).; The Supabase Auth configuration itself (OTP expiry, signup enablement, MFA) — not reachable over SQL and no MCP tool for it was used.; Resend/Twilio/Stripe dashboard-side settings (allowed senders, webhook endpoints, key scopes).; Whether the 9 never-used internal credentials are referenced by any live caller (no call-site trace; use_count 0 is the only evidence).; Content of the 70,626 kb_chunk_quarantine rows beyond the count (what else was quarantined and why).; Full body of the drawing_redact/#566 change (project-identity masking of legends) — only the SQL header was read; effectiveness not tested because the portals are known incomplete and production holds 0 inquiries.; The 20+ probe/rehearsal SQL files that both read leads/customers and insert into notifications were only grep-classified; each insert's recipient expression was not individually verified against the fixture convention.

**Findings**

- **BLOCKS REVENUE** — The sales_engineer has never run against a real customer. Its 105 runs all fall inside 2026-09-09 01:07–02:53 UTC (one session); 0 inquiries were created in the last 30 days; the only persisted match (inquiry_matches upsert, persisted=1 twice) targets inquiry 464035ce-… which no longer exists in public.inquiries. The seat has a runtime but no scheduled trigger — it is reached only by customer_api on a new inquiry (action=match) and by PortalShell (action=assist), and the customer portal is known incomplete.
  - evidence: agent_runs agent='sales_engineer' min/max started_at 2026-09-09 01:07:41 / 02:53:29; select count(*) from inquiries where created_at >= now()-'30 days' = 0; select from inquiries where id='464035ce-8846-49df-9789-c4470f885f73' = []; resilience_ping calls no sales routine; customer_api/index.ts L205; PortalShell.tsx L95
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — The CEO seat has never decided, accepted or closed anything. ceo_approval has 0 rows; both support_ceo_report rows addressed to it (accountant weekly report, 9 Sep) sit at accepted_at NULL; the only two ceo_command/ceo_task rows are the founder's 8 Sep test command ('build the ceo edge function and verify it end to end'), still state 'open' with no evidence. Nothing in production calls functions/v1/ceo_agent (0 pg_proc bodies, 0 src files; only a CI check and a probe). The seat's only live output is the daily digest email, which is fn_founder_brief in SQL, not the CEO runtime.
  - evidence: ceo_approval count 0; support_ceo_report ids 138/140 accepted_at NULL; ceo_task ids 12/13 state open; agent_runs has no agent='ceo' row; grep of pg_proc.prosrc for functions/v1/ceo_agent = 0 rows; org_role.ceo.runtime_mode='scheduled_manager' but no scheduler invokes ceo_agent
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — org_tasks has no consumer. All 13 non-cancelled tasks assigned to accountant (3), customer_support (3) and sales_engineer (7) have attempts=0 and last_run_at NULL since 9–10 Sep. The routines that could close them (fn_org_task_deterministic_done, fn_org_task_close_as_fact, fn_org_task_promote) are not in the resilience_ping call list, and no seat function reads org_tasks. Seven customer/supplier questions the sales_engineer could not answer and three the widget could not answer are queued to nobody.
  - evidence: select id,assigned_agent,status,attempts,last_run_at from public.org_tasks — 13 rows status 'assigned', attempts 0, last_run_at NULL; rpc list in supabase/functions/resilience_ping/index.ts contains no org_task routine
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — The customer_support seat has answered only test traffic and two of its three doors have never carried a legitimate message. Website widget: 41 assistant_logs all on 9 Sep, including 'charter a helicopter to the moon' and nonsense strings. WhatsApp: both wa_inbound runs in 30d were rejected for invalid Twilio HMAC signature (one a probe, one on 10 Sep 03:17 from an unknown sender), 1 wa_messages row in 30d. Email: email_triage has never returned ok (1 warn 403) and still calls makeLlm — the OpenRouter hold binds here. supplier_response_watch, the evidence table this seat owns for every non-response refund, has 0 rows ever.
  - evidence: assistant_logs 41 rows max(created_at) 2026-09-09 21:16; agent_runs wa_inbound 2 rows status error counts signature_valid 0; wa_messages 30d count 1; fn:email_triage 1 row HTTP 403; supplier_response_watch count 0
- **Degrades** — The CEO weekly report for W36 was generated (9 Sep 17:47, manual call) and never delivered: delivery_attempts 0, founder_submitted_at NULL. fn_ceo_weekly_report_tick only fires on isodow 1 at ≥08:00 Dubai and always computes the PREVIOUS week's key (v_ws = date_trunc('week', today-7)), so on Monday 14 Sep it will build W37 and the W36 row will remain 'generated' forever; nothing retries an undelivered prior week.
  - evidence: public.ceo_weekly_report id 1: status 'generated', delivery_attempts 0, notification_id NULL; prosrc of fn_ceo_weekly_report_tick: 'already delivered' guard checks only founder_submitted_at for the current v_key; app_secrets ceo_weekly_report_local_dow=1, local_time=08:00
- **Degrades** — The accountant's refund path has only ever routed fixtures. Its 3 'ok' refund_request runs (9 Sep 03:59–04:01) point at support_ceo_report ids 130 and 131, which no longer exist (the table holds only 138 and 140). No real refund has ever been placed before the CEO, and fn_ceo_request_decision's output is a support_ceo_report the CEO has never accepted.
  - evidence: agent_runs ids 57861/57866/57867 counts report_id 130/131; select id from support_ceo_report → 138, 140 only; ceo_approval 0 rows
- **Degrades** — A rate-limit refusal was recorded as a knowledge gap and assigned to customer_support as if it were an unanswered question: org_tasks 578947 ('كم تكلفة تركيب مصعد', REFUSAL: rate_limited) was created after site_assistant's per-IP limiter (10/hour) fired four times on 9 Sep 18:02–20:47 during testing. The gap queue cannot tell a throttled caller from an unsourced answer.
  - evidence: org_tasks id 578947 detail contains 'REFUSAL: rate_limited'; agent_runs site_assistant 4 rows status error counts {scope:'ip', rate_limited:1}
- **Cosmetic** — The accountant and it_engineer edge functions have no production caller; their real work runs as SQL ticks inside resilience_ping and (for receipts) inside resend_inbound. functions/v1/accountant is invoked only by a CI check (scripts/checks/every_expense_record_can_be_opened.test.mjs → 85 'document' runs on 9 Sep) and probes; functions/v1/it_engineer accepts only a founder JWT (admin_emails) and ran 12 times from a probe. Both seats produce real outcomes (W36 financial report delivered with PDF; 18 IT handover emails), but the edge functions the org_role calls their 'runtime' are not on the path.
  - evidence: grep -rl 'functions/v1/accountant\|it_engineer' over scripts/sql, src, pg_proc.prosrc → 0 production sites; it_engineer/index.ts L60-66 identity = admin_emails only; accountant_weekly_report id 3 delivered_at 2026-09-09 04:39:55; notifications it_engineer_handover 18 sent
- **Cosmetic** — ceo_daily_digest.delivered_at is never set: all 5 digest rows show delivered=NULL although their notification_id rows are status 'sent'. No routine in pg_proc references both ceo_daily_digest and delivered_at. A column that reads as 'undelivered' on every row that was delivered is a false signal for whoever audits the digest.
  - evidence: select from ceo_daily_digest: delivered_at NULL x5, notification_id 21285/21405/21408/21411/21413 all status sent; pg_proc prosrc ~* 'ceo_daily_digest' and ~* 'delivered_at' → 0 rows
- **Cosmetic** — org_role.ceo still carries pre-7-Sep vocabulary: handoff_targets [chief_of_staff, chief_performance_officer] (seats that do not exist in org_role), responsibilities 'reviews department performance', owned_decisions 'capital allocation between departments', and 8 tools (bi_snapshot, growth_simulate, exec_review…) that no edge function implements. The other four seats have tools=[] or three names that are also not actions on any function.
  - evidence: org_role row ceo: handoff_targets, tools, responsibilities as read; org_role has exactly 5 role_ids; ceo_agent actions are command/context/inbox/board/accept/task/decide — none of the 8 tool names

*Not measured:* Supabase edge-function invocation logs (analytics) — not queried; call counts here come only from agent_runs, which observe.ts throttles for fast OK runs; Vercel cron execution history for /api/resilience-ping — inferred from fn:resilience_ping rows and it_watch_tick cadence, not from Vercel; Whether the five founder_brief and 18 it_engineer_handover emails were actually delivered by Resend — only notifications.status='sent' was read; Argument-name agreement between the TS rpc call sites and the SQL signatures for 36 of the 49 routines (existence and overload count checked for all 49; signatures read for 13); The full bodies of fn_it_detect, fn_accountant_weekly_report_tick, fn_ceo_daily_digest (generator) and fn_sales_assist — write targets taken from regex over prosrc plus row evidence, so a write via EXECUTE or a nested routine would be missed; Whether the widget has had zero visitors since 9 Sep or whether ChatWidget is failing silently — assistant_logs is empty after 9 Sep 21:16 and the site was not driven; Origin of the 10 Sep 03:17 wa_inbound signature-rejected webhook (real Twilio traffic vs scan); agent_tool (10 rows) and agent_tool_call (22 rows) — not mapped to seats; History before 2026-09-07 18:15 — agent_runs has no older rows, so no seat's behaviour before that date can be measured from it; The customer /me and supplier portals were not driven, per the founder's instruction

**Findings**

- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — The kb embedding backfill is dead: the only remaining trigger (GitHub daily workflow) fails every run because scripts/kb/run_embed_backfill.mjs was never committed (untracked in git), and the in-DB fn_kb_embed_tick has no caller by design. 2,437,587 of 2,615,400 chunks (93.2%) remain unembedded; pg_net shows zero embed traffic in 24h. The knowledge 'brain' the founder asked for on 9 Sep is not being built.
  - evidence: gh run list --workflow kb-embed-backfill.yml: failure 10,11,12,13 Sep; failed-step log: Cannot find module .../scripts/kb/run_embed_backfill.mjs; git ls-files --error-unmatch scripts/kb/run_embed_backfill.mjs -> not known to git; job count step: embedded 177,813 of 2,615,400 (6.80%); kb_embed_inflight 0, net._http_response 0 rows/24h
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY · founder** — The five seat runtimes (ceo_agent, it_engineer, accountant, sales_engineer, customer_support via site_assistant) have no scheduled entry point; everything autonomous the company does is the SQL inside the eleven ticks. All seat edge functions show 0 invocations in the last 24h and last ran on 9 Sep. This is by design under the OpenRouter hold (noted once; not a recommendation to raise it) but means the company acts only when a human or a tick's SQL acts.
  - evidence: agent_runs: accountant last 9 Sep 22:50, sales_engineer 9 Sep 02:53, it_engineer 9 Sep 06:53, fn:ceo_agent 9 Sep 20:06, site_assistant 9 Sep 21:16; resilience_ping/index.ts calls no seat edge function
- **Degrades** — The scheduler's own failure signal is permanently red: the Vercel route aborts at 30 s (AbortSignal.timeout(30_000)) while resilience_ping now averages 30.7 s (11 serial ticks), so 61 of 96 cron invocations in 24h returned 502. The edge function still completes (all 97 agent_runs ok), but the 502 was designed to be the platform-log sign of a dead watchdog and now fires on 64% of healthy ticks; a real failure is indistinguishable. The GitHub daily curl uses the same 30 s cap and succeeded on 4/4 observed days only by timing.
  - evidence: Vercel get_runtime_logs group_by statusCode 24h: 502=61, 200=35; agent_runs fn:resilience_ping ms avg 30,742, 54/97 > 30,000; src/app/api/resilience-ping/route.ts line with AbortSignal.timeout(30_000); .github/workflows/resilience-ping.yml curl -m 30
- **Degrades** — The scheduler-down page cannot page: resilience_ping's unhealthy branch POSTs to /alert_founder, which is not in the repo and not deployed (67 deployed functions listed; alert_founder absent). paged will be false on every future outage; only the send_outbox flush fallback survives, and no alert row is queued for it to flush. Additionally fn_cron_liveness measures it_watch_tick, which resilience_ping itself writes, so the Vercel path can never observe its own death; only the once-daily GitHub curl can reach the branch.
  - evidence: resilience_ping/index.ts lines 476-482; list_edge_functions (no alert_founder); ls supabase/functions (no alert_founder); founder_alerts last SCHEDULER DOWN row 2026-09-07 23:15, i.e. before commit 182308b deleted the function on 8 Sep; fn_cron_liveness prosrc reads public.it_watch_tick offset 1
- **Degrades** — fn_dropbox_backup_due reports due=true for the whole day whenever there is nothing to upload, because 'ran_today' looks for an agent_runs row with agent='dropbox_backup' and the edge function only writes that row when it uploads something (idle runs are not logged). Result: dropbox_backup is invoked up to 97 times per day (4.1 s each) instead of once, and the IT engineer's run_silent detector learned a 15-minute cadence from it, then emailed the founder [HIGH] and [RESOLVED] on 12 Sep when a real upload at 06:30 stopped the calls for the rest of the day -- a self-inflicted alarm.
  - evidence: fn_dropbox_backup_due prosrc (agent='dropbox_backup' and status='ok' today); dropbox_backup/index.ts logRun/logThrottled write agent='dropbox_backup' only on signal; fn:dropbox_backup per day 97/43/85 vs 'dropbox_backup' rows 3 in 5 days; fn_dropbox_backup_due() at 20:52 UTC: due=true ran_today=false queued=0; it_watch_finding run_silent dropbox_backup opened 12 Sep 07:30 resolved 20:00 seen 51; it_engineer_handover emails 12 Sep 07:30 and 20:00
- **Degrades** — The CEO weekly report W36 is orphaned: it was generated 9 Sep 17:47 (status generated, founder_submitted_at NULL, 0 delivery attempts) and the tick, when it first fires on Mon 14 Sep 04:00 UTC, will compute report_key W37 and never look at W36 again (its 'already delivered' check is keyed by the new week and it has no retry path for an undelivered prior week, unlike the accountant tick which does).
  - evidence: ceo_weekly_report row quotesdone:2026-W36; fn_ceo_weekly_report_tick prosrc: v_ws := date_trunc('week', v_local::date - 7), exists(... report_key = v_key and founder_submitted_at is not null) only; compare fn_accountant_weekly_report_tick which retries when delivered_at is null
- **Degrades** — Credential rotation no longer exists anywhere: pg_cron job 334 is gone and the routines fn_internal_credential_rotate / _rotate_due named in scripts/sql/action_invoker.sql are not in the database. 60 of 85 active credentials carry an expiry; the first is 2027-08-03. Memory 'credentials rotate themselves' is stale.
  - evidence: pg_proc proname ilike '%rotat%' -> 0 rows; cron.job 1 row (wa_flush); internal_credential aggregate: 85 active, 60 with expires_at, min 2027-08-03, 1 ever rotated
- **Degrades · founder** — Two deployed edge functions whose only purpose is scheduled have no trigger: email_cron (Resend domain-verification detect, formerly every 2 min) and wa_templates_watch (Twilio template-approval poll). Their last runs are the 9 Sep probe sweep. send_outbox is now driven directly by the tick, so only email_cron's verification-detect job is lost; wa_templates_watch is the one thing that would notice the WhatsApp template being approved.
  - evidence: call-site census (src, supabase/functions, scripts/sql) = 0 for both; cron.job empty; agent_runs fn:email_cron 1 run 9 Sep 07:10 (error), fn:wa_templates_watch 1 run 9 Sep 07:11 (warn). Boundary: the census does not see site calls built from src/lib/supaEnv.ts SUPA_FN template, but neither function is a site surface (secret-gated / verify_jwt)
- **Degrades** — Three ticks are wired but have never been proven through the scheduler: ratingTick (0 transactions exist, so no candidate ever), ceoWeeklyTick and accountantWeeklyTick (configured for Monday; no Monday has passed since 9 Sep; the one accountant report was produced at 04:39:55, not a tick timestamp). The first live test of both weekly ticks is Mon 14 Sep 04:00/05:00 UTC.
  - evidence: transactions count 0; app_secrets *_local_dow=1; accountant_weekly_report.delivered_at 2026-09-09 04:39:55; ceo_weekly_report founder_submitted_at NULL
- **Cosmetic** — Two DB routines fire at targets that do not exist: fn_sanctions_screen_tick posts to edge sanctions_screen (not in repo, not deployed) and fn_os_incident_open posts to alert_founder (same). Neither routine has a caller, so nothing errors today, but the weekly supplier sanctions screening the compliance SQL describes has no runtime at either end.
  - evidence: pg_proc prosrc regexp functions/v1/(sanctions_screen\|alert_founder); list_edge_functions lacks both; pg_proc/pg_trigger search finds no caller of either routine

*Not measured:* Whether Vercel sets CRON_SECRET (route accepts unauthenticated calls if unset) -- not readable without Vercel env access; Whether the Supabase edge runtime ever kills resilience_ping after the Vercel client aborts at 30 s -- evidence (all 97 runs status ok, all ticks' tables advancing) says it completes, but no run was traced end to end; Per-tick success inside a resilience_ping response body: the body is not logged; tick health was inferred from each tick's own output table (it_watch_tick, openrouter_meter, ceo_daily_digest, etc.), not from the JSON returned; fn_it_detect internals (50 KB) were not read; only its outputs and its reference to fn_backup_watch were checked; fn_support_request_ratings_due beyond its first 2,600 chars; Vercel function maxDuration for the route (plan is pro; the observed 502s come from the route's own AbortSignal, not a platform kill); Site-side callers of edge functions: src builds URLs from SUPA_FN (src/lib/supaEnv.ts), so the zero-caller census is reliable only for functions that are secret-gated/internal; customer_api, supplier_api, login_otp etc. are demonstrably called (agent_runs 20:38-20:42 UTC) and were not counted; Whether the 26 abandoned doc_backups (supplier_missing, July) should ever be retried; The two GitHub daily workflows' exact command output (only conclusion=success was read); Whether founder_mail_policy would allow the IT engineer's next email kinds -- 18 were delivered, so the path works for that kind only; Anything behind the customer /me or supplier portal UI (out of scope per the founder)

**Findings**

- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — The founder's WhatsApp command channel is broken end to end: wa_inbound calls fn_wa_context('founder') which throws on fn_founder_queue (missing), then POSTs the question to /agent_brain, a function that exists neither in the repo nor on production. Every founder question returns 'agent_brain returned no reply (HTTP 404)'. site_assistant also calls fn_wa_context (non-founder audiences work).
  - evidence: supabase/functions/wa_inbound/index.ts:620 and :626; list_edge_functions has no agent_brain; select fn_wa_context('founder','+971500000000') -> 42883
- **BLOCKS AUTONOMY** — resilience_ping's 'SCHEDULER DOWN — automation stalled' critical alert posts to /alert_founder, which is deployed nowhere. The one alarm meant to fire when automation stops cannot reach anyone.
  - evidence: supabase/functions/resilience_ping/index.ts:480; slug absent from repo and from list_edge_functions
- **Degrades** — The seats' read tool fn_query_platform returns an error object for topics exec, governance and operations because fn_ws_exec/fn_ws_governance/fn_ws_operations call fn_founder_queue and fn_founder_refund_approvals_open, both dropped when founder approvals were removed. The wrapper's exception handler masks the throw as {error:'query_failed'}.
  - evidence: select fn_query_platform('exec','{}') -> query_failed / fn_founder_queue does not exist; _shared/tools.ts exposes fn_query_platform
- **Degrades** — 16 production routines call 16 routines that do not exist and therefore throw on every call; 13 of them are reachable only from other dead code, but they are still deployed and will be found by any auditor running the same scan. fn_agent_context and fn_agent_context_v3 (the pre-Sep context builders) are the largest: 7 missing callees between them.
  - evidence: pg_proc scan; callers listed in what_exists; none attached as trigger or cron
- **Degrades** — Two live triggers name a remedy that does not exist: zzz_fn_notifications_demo_guard (on notifications) instructs 'Release with fn_notifications_release_held()' and trg_scenario_expected_needs_ledger (on kb_stage.scenario_tests) instructs 'Use fn_scenario_expectation_change(...)'. An operator following the hint hits 42883.
  - evidence: prosrc of both trigger functions; pg_proc has neither callee in any schema
- **Degrades** — supplier_api still fetches three phantom edge functions (profile_ai, supplier_review, capability_agent) inside try/catch; each call 404s and the feature silently yields nothing.
  - evidence: supabase/functions/supplier_api/index.ts:123,126,312; slugs absent from repo and production
- **Degrades** — 19 active check files in scripts/checks are plain .mjs that no workflow, package.json script, runner or test imports — they cannot go red. checks.yml already records this failure class twice (attachment.test.ts, ssrf_guard) and it has recurred.
  - evidence: run.mjs discovers *.test.mjs only; grep of each basename across .github, package.json, run.mjs, *.test.mjs, scripts/*.mjs = 0 references for 19 files
- **Degrades** — docs/ is 88% pre-7-Sep and describes an organisation that no longer exists: 164 files last touched before the department removal mention departments; docs/agents carries a ten-department role registry, committee graph and workflow ownership; docs/phases carries PHASE1/PHASE2 plans; only three subjects have a RETIRED_* notice; there is no index. An auditor cannot tell current from historical without git blame.
  - evidence: git log -1 per file; grep -rli department docs; ls docs/agents docs/phases; no docs/README.md
- **Cosmetic** — Historical debris is committed at the root and in odd directories: 5.8 MB repomix-src.xml and 2.7 MB QUOTESDONE_CODEBASE.md snapshots, a 0-byte prod_def.sql, scratchpad/q.mjs, _archive/OsApp.tsx.bak, holdout_v2_eval/ (tarball, .cmd files, another repomix), a directory named after a Phase D spec, and SYNCED_FROM_PRODUCTION.md that lists functions no longer existing. The working tree additionally holds 760 untracked debris paths (261 at root) from the redirect-rewriting hook.
  - evidence: git ls-files; stat; git status --porcelain
- **Cosmetic** — 69 routines (incl. 24 fn_os_* dashboard actions and 12 unattached trigger functions) have no reference in the database or the repository, and DROPPED.txt already carries 600 names the repo defines but production lacks — the estate's routine layer is roughly one part live to one part residue.
  - evidence: 603 candidate routines; 201 unreferenced in DB; 69 unreferenced anywhere; DROPPED.txt 600 lines
- **Cosmetic** — RLS is on for all 471 tables but only 26 have policies and only 3 of those are reachable by a client role; 23 tables carry policies no client can reach. The 445 policy-less tables are safe today only because nothing grants anon/authenticated SELECT — the guard is the grant, not the policy, and the advisor will keep reporting 495 findings.
  - evidence: pg_policies x has_table_privilege; get_advisors security
- **Cosmetic** — leads (22,264 rows) carries 46 columns that are null in every sampled row (bank/IBAN, Emirates ID, VAT, ISO, insurance, registration/subscription payment, licence gate reasons); supplier_capabilities 14; supplier_score 3 (avg_response_hours, price_index, quality_rating never computed). Schema promises data no writer produces — much of it belongs to the parked supplier portal.
  - evidence: pg_stats null_frac=1 on tables >= 20 rows
- **Cosmetic** — Clean: scripts/sql is exactly indexed (997 = 997, 0 gaps, 0 dupes), edge functions are 1:1 with production, all 460 SECURITY DEFINER routines pin search_path, 0 broken relative links in docs, founder_instruction exists with status and evidence columns. The MANIFEST header's prose count (852) is the one stale number in an otherwise exact register.
  - evidence: measurements above
- **Cosmetic · founder** — Where the OpenRouter hold binds in this dimension: the phantom agent_brain/profile_ai/supplier_review/capability_agent slugs were the LLM-runtime functions; their removal is the reason the callers 404. Noted once; not a recommendation to raise the hold.
  - evidence: SYNCED_FROM_PRODUCTION.md lists agent_brain v18; callers still fetch it

*Not measured:* Whether the 12 uncalled webhook/OTP edge functions are wired at the provider side (Resend, Twilio, Stripe, Meta) — external configuration, not readable from repo or DB; What schedules the seat tick routines (fn_*_tick, fn_*_deliver) and email_cron/email_triage now that pg_cron is inactive — traced only to resilience_ping's 4 fan-outs; did not read every seat's index.ts; Performance advisors (only the security advisor was fetched); the 561-key baseline vs today's 543 was not reconciled key by key; Dead columns on tables with fewer than 20 rows, and columns null by sampling artefact — pg_stats is a sample, not a full scan; Views read only by humans in the Supabase dashboard or by MCP sessions; Routines invoked only from the Supabase dashboard, SQL editor or memory-file recipes (the 69 'no reference anywhere' count is a repo+DB measurement); Docs content accuracy file by file — only date, department/phase mentions and link integrity were measured; which of the 28 post-7-Sep department-mentioning docs are deliberately historical was not read; The 37 unpinned-search_path routines' actual exposure (they are non-definer; risk depends on caller role) — names listed, not assessed; Whether the 19 unrun check files still pass if executed — not executed, some hit production; Edge functions' internal rpc targets (122 names) against pg_proc — not diffed; a missing rpc target would 404 at runtime; Both portals' behaviour — excluded by the founder's standing rule; scripts/staging (123 files) and scripts/kb (65 files) currency

## 4. What is still to come in Part 2

- Adversarial verification of every finding in §1 (three lenses: is the number right; does it actually block; was it already fixed) — this was running when paused, with sixteen verifiers in flight.
- The verdict on which seats are missing, which need rebuilding, and which the founder's refusals (HR, product manager, procurement) still hold against.
- The one-year plan: per seat, per quarter, exact steps, done-when measurable in the database; the knowledge programme that takes each seat from its measured depth to senior-human reasoning; the target communication workflow.
- The first 30 days, day by day.
